
FILE docs/cod4/request.txt
    1 # Dynasty cod4.txt —— 新增内容（与现有任务/装备/成长体系完整融合）
    2 
    3 > 本文件负责**新增内容**，但一切新增都先查重、复用现有 Item/Weapon/Ability/Damage/Animation/Task 体系，不造第二套系统。
    4 > 原则优先级：现有内容深化 > 重复创造；现有任务联动 > 新造任务系统；真实玩法机制 > 属性堆叠；客户端视觉 + 服务端权威逻辑 > 大量服务器视觉实体；Registry 兼容 > 改 ID；中国神话/王朝 > 泛奇幻。
    5 >
    6 > **查重基准（本文件已扫过，勿重复）**：
    7 > - cod1 基础怪（已在/正在实现）：卒伍刀手、拒马长枪兵、连弩阵卒、橹盾甲士、符法祭酒、铁索斥候、溃军死士、阵亡掌旗官、山精山魈 + 21 只（cod1 第 9~30 号）；cod1 精英 15、Boss 12、神器 25、神影 12。
    8 > - 已实现实体：terracotta_warrior / imperial_soldier / minister / assassin / archer / royal_guard / rebel_soldier / nian_beast / qilin / phoenix / nine_tailed_fox / jade_guard / soul_soldier / thunder_envoy / merfolk + Boss（dragon_emperor 等 6 + zhenyuan_sovereign）。
    9 > - 已实现武器 ~50、盔甲 28 套、饰品 ~89、效果 7、药水 6、附魔（BREAKER/BEHEADING/BULWARK 等）、材料（Relics/FineItems/Items）、食物（BasicFoods/FineItems）。
   10 
   11 ================================================================================
   12 COD4-1 新增 30 只次级小怪（轻于 cod1 核心怪；只补世界密度/材料/环境）
   13 ================================================================================
   14 
   15 ## 1.0 定位与通用标准
   16 
   17 - 定位比 cod1 核心怪**更轻**：世界密度、小规模战斗、材料来源、环境特色。**不推翻 cod2 生态**，只提供 Spawn Hook。
   18 - spawn 只提供 Hook（biome/时间/天气/结构 tag），不重写 cod2 生态。
   19 - 通用骨骼：人形用分段真实骨架（pelvis/waist/chest/neck/head/upper_arm/elbow/forearm/wrist/hand/thigh/knee/shin/ankle/foot）；兽形用「头/颈/躯/尾/四足」5 段；飞行用「头/身/双翼/尾」；水生用「头/身/鳍/尾」。
   20 - 通用 AI 状态机：`idle / wander / hunt / flee / attack / special / evade`；无寻路负担（次级怪）时用低阶 Goal，不堆复杂战术。
   21 - 通用性能：次级怪面数 ≤ 基础怪，同屏 ≤ 20 只不卡；掉率低、掉落轻；无 Boss 条。
   22 - 通用 multiplayer：次级怪为常规敌对/中立，无共享 Credit 问题；掉落由击杀者拾取，不重复。
   23 
   24 ## 1.1 30 只表
   25 
   26 > 属性「血/攻/速/护甲」为推荐初始值（可调范围 ±20%）；「掉落」标注用途（材料需 ≥2 用途，见 COD4-11）。

   61 ## 1.2 类型分布核对（满足分散要求）
   62

  109 COD4-4 现有盔甲套装特效完善（28 套，保留原 ID/身份，加行为型 2/3/4 件）
  110 ================================================================================
  111 
  112 > 现有套装只有纯数值（减伤/生命/护甲，`DynastyBalance.SET_BONUS`）。本次**保留原套装与 ID**，在数值之上补 2/3/4 件行为。原则：2 件=小型便利/战斗行为；3 件=改变战斗节奏；4 件=核心行为机制。**至少一半必须涉及行为**（格挡/蓄势/连击/夜战/水战/站桩/空中/特殊资源），不许全写 +X% 属性。

  145 ## 4.1 行为实现约定
  146 
  147 - 2/3/4 件判定复用 `DynastySetBonus.wornArmorIds()` 每秒 refresh；摘下立即回退，无幽灵加成。
  148 - 行为型效果优先走「触发事件」（完美格挡=ShieldBlockEvent 时间窗、连击=DynastyTrinketOnHit 同源连击、站桩=位移阈值计时、夜战=isDay、水战=isUnderWater、空中=fallDistance、怒气=受击计数）。
  149 - 数值为推荐初始值，可调范围 ±30%；Boss 免伤/免疫效果设长 CD（≥300s），不破坏 Boss 战。
  150 - **禁止**强迫整套才能用武器；系列联动是加深 Build，不是门槛（见 COD4-5）。
  151

  174 COD4-6 现有饰品完善（不新建 20 个，只改现有）

  176 
  177 ## 6.0 判定（对现有 ~89 件抽查，标 GOOD/TOO_SIMPLE/DUPLICATE/NO_SOURCE/NO_TASK_LINK/NO_VISUAL）
  178 
  179 | 判定 | 含义 | 处理 |
  180 |---|---|---|
  181 | GOOD | 机制/来源/视觉都够 | 保留 |
  182 | TOO_SIMPLE | 只有 +X% 属性 | 补机制或视觉 |
  183 | DUPLICATE | 与别件重复 | 差异化或合并用途 |
  184 | NO_SOURCE | 无掉落/合成来源 | 接任务/掉落/配方 |
  185 | NO_TASK_LINK | 无任务关联 | 挂现有 FTB 任务 |
  186 | NO_VISUAL | 无视觉反馈 | 接 COD3-6 视觉 |
  187

  212 
  213 ## 6.2 原则
  214 
  215 - 不无脑加属性；每个「改成」至少含 trigger 或视觉或来源之一。
  216 - 不叠层（stacking）：同件/同槽不重复生效；incompatibility 写清互斥。
  217 - 所有饰品来源：优先复用现有合成/掉落/官阶/任务/秘密，**不新建掉落表**除非确实无来源。
  218 
  219 ================================================================================
  220 COD4-7 装备/饰品 ↔ 现有任务系统联动（真实接入，非一句话）
  221 ================================================================================
  222 
  223 ## 7.0 现有任务系统结构（已扫）
  224 
  225 - 载体：FTB Quests `.snbt` 章节（`dynasty_c1..c13` 材料/合成、`dynasty_story_01..08` 主线、`dynasty_accessory_necklace/charm/belt/hands/ring/body/back/bracelet` 按槽位、`dynasty_build_guard/sword/archer/talisman` 流派、`dynasty_boss_ascent`、`dynasty_boss_guides`、`dynasty_home`、`dynasty_legacy`）。
  226 - 任务类型（实际存在）：`checkmark`（阅读页）、`item`（计数检测，不收取）、`xp`、`command`（如 `dynasty merit 12`）。
  227 - 奖励类型（实际存在）：`item`、`xp`、`command`。
  228 - 自动判定：`DynastyAdvancements.award(...)` → Advancement → FTB 自动完成；另有 `DynastyBossCombat` 首杀、`DynastyWeaponGifts` 条件授武、`DynastyWeaponProgression` 击杀成长、`DynastyAccessoryRefining` 淬炼里程碑。
  229 
  230 ## 7.1 通用规则（所有装备任务联动都遵循）
  231 
  232 | 项 | 约定 |
  233 |---|---|
  234 | Objective 复用 | 优先复用等价 Objective（item 检测 / advancement / command）；不新建第二套 Task 系统。 |
  235 | 奖励方式 | 默认**不直接塞背包**；优先「解锁配方 / 解锁 Boss 额外掉落 / 解锁地下城宝箱池 / 解锁 NPC 商店 / 解锁锻造 / 解锁饰品升级」。 |
  236 | 是否一次性 | 装备类任务默认一次性（重复做不给第二件）。 |
  237 | 多人 Credit | 击杀类用 `getKillCredit()`（现有首杀逻辑）；采集/合成类各自完成。 |
  238 | Restart/掉线 | 进度存玩家持久数据（persistentData）/ Advancement；重启不掉。 |
  239 | 旧存档兼容 | 已获得装备但任务未完成：补发 advancement（`RitualQuestProgress.restore` 模式）；已完成任务旧存档：不动。 |
  240 
  241 ## 7.2 联动示例（逐条，含 Task/前置/Objective/Hook/奖励方式）
  242 
  243 | 装备/饰品 | 对应现有任务(章) | 前置 | Objective | 进度 Hook | 奖励方式 | 一次性 | 旧档兼容 |
  244 |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  245 | jade_pendant | dynasty_accessory_necklace | c1 玉料 | item: jade_pendant ×1 | 拾取检测（onFirstObtain） | 解锁「玉饰」合成页（JEI） | 是 | 已拥有→补 advancement |
  246 | heart_mirror | dynasty_accessory_charm | 铜镜 | item: heart_mirror ×1 | 合成/拾取 | 解锁「铜镜」商人补货 | 是 | 同上 |
  247 | jade_crown | dynasty_story_01 | 官阶 rank≥3 | advancement: rank_scholar | checkDerived | 御赐 jade_crown（command 发放） | 是 | 已 3 阶→补发 |
  248 | silk_pouch | dynasty_home | 丝绸 | item: silk_pouch ×1 | 拾取 | 解锁 +1 饰品槽（复用 RankPerks） | 是 | 同上 |
  249 | dragon_scale_charm | dynasty_boss_ascent | 击败龙帝 | advancement: slay_dragon_emperor | DynastyBossCombat 首杀 | 解锁「龙鳞佩」配方 | 是 | 已杀→补 |
  250 | sea_pearl | dynasty_story_02 | 进龙宫 | advancement: entered_dragon_palace | DynastyWorldEvents 维度检测 | 解锁龙宫商人卖 sea_pearl | 是 | 已进→补 |
  251 | 学院武器（8 把） | dynasty_build_guard/sword/archer/talisman | 对应流派入门 | advancement: school_*_rank_1/3 | DynastySchoolProgression | 解锁流派武器合成 + 流派饰品 | 是 | 已达标→补 |
  252 | 四象武器（xuanwu_blade 等） | dynasty_boss_ascent | 四象祭坛 | advancement: ritual_qinglong/baihu/zhuque/xuanwu | RitualQuestProgress | 解锁四象武器锻造 | 是 | 已献祭→补 |
  253 | 新弩（repeating_crossbow/siege_crossbow） | dynasty_build_archer | 弓流 rank≥6 | advancement: school_archer_rank_6 | DynastySchoolProgression | 解锁弩配方 | 是 | 已 6→补 |
  254 | 新奇门（rope_dart/meteor_hammer 等） | dynasty_build_guard 或 story | 军功 rank | advancement / command merit | DynastyMerit | 解锁奇门商人 | 是 | 已达标→补 |
  255 
  256 ## 7.3 原则重申
  257 
  258 - 任务 = 内容解锁 + 引导层，**不是单纯发奖按钮**。
  259 - 成长逻辑：任务出现在什么阶段 → 玩家当时有什么装备 → 奖励解决什么 Build 需求 → 多久被替代 → 被替代后有无继续用途（升级/锻造/系列联动/特定场景优势）。
  260 - 找不到对应现有任务时：写 `NO_CURRENT_TASK_LINK`，并建议在哪个现有任务分支加一个 Stage/Objective，**不因此新建一条主线**（见 COD4-15 大表）。
  261

  332 
  333 ================================================================================
  334 COD4-12 全局战斗反馈（Normal/Heavy/Critical/Block/Perfect Block/Armor Break/Stagger/Knockdown/Launch/Boss Immune）
  335 ================================================================================
  336 
  337 | 类型 | VFX | SFX | 动画反馈 | 禁止 |
  338 |---|---|---|---|---|
  339 | Normal Hit | 命中火花（材质色） | 短促命中 | 目标轻微后仰 0.1s | 无 |
  340 | Heavy Hit | 冲击波小环 | 低沉重击 | 目标后仰 0.2s | 不震屏 |
  341 | Critical | 金星/星纹 + 数字放大 | 脆响 | 目标明显后仰 | 不闪白 |
  342 | Block | 盾牌/武器火星 + 盾牌纹 | 金属格挡 | 举盾定格 | 无 |
  343 | Perfect Block | 金环 + 时停感 0.1s | 清脆「铛」 | 完美格挡后反击前摇 | 不锁镜头 |
  344 | Armor Break | 甲碎裂纹 decal + 碎片 | 碎裂 | 目标踉跄 0.2s | 无 |
  345 | Stagger | 脚下晃影 + 踉跄 | 闷哼 | 破势后摇 | 不强制转向 |
  346 | Knockdown | 倒地尘土 + 目标倒地 | 重摔 | 倒地→起身 | 不震屏 |
  347 | Launch | 上扬尘土 + 目标浮空 | 破空 | 浮空 0.4s 落地 | 不强制抬头 |
  348 | Boss Immune | 「免疫」灰字 + 弹开微光 | 嗡鸣 | Boss 纹丝不动 | 不嘲讽刷屏 |
  349 
  350 - 全局禁止：强制 Camera 转向、大幅持续震屏、全屏纯白。
  351 - 反馈由服务端决定「类型 + 目标」广播，客户端播放；不改变他人 Camera。
  352

    1 # Dynasty cod4.txt —— 剩余任务清单
    2 
    3 整理日期：2026-10-08（北京时间）
    4 核对仓库：gyhchang-cell/dynasty
    5 核对分支及提交：cod4 / 47b19ef59ff8e2f16e055e22d5f53d43ff94e18d
    6 目标版本：Minecraft 1.20.1 Forge
    7 
    8 说明：
    9 - 已从原清单删除能够确认完成的开发要求；部分完成的条目只保留剩余部分。
   10 - “待补齐”表示目前仍有实现缺口或与原要求不一致；“待核对”表示尚缺足够的完成证据；“待验证”表示已有实现，但尚未完成实际游戏验收。
   11 - 保留原章节编号，因此编号可以不连续。已完成的注册、基础框架、常规配方、已通过的专项测试不再列为开发任务。
   12 - 本清单不是要求重做现有功能。继续工作前查重，复用当前 Item/Weapon/Ability/Damage/Animation/Task、COD2/COD3 接口，保留 Registry ID 与存档兼容。
   13 - 不新造任务主线，不强制整套装备才能用武器，不使用强制转镜头、持续震屏或全屏白闪。
   14

   24 ## 1.2 待核对、待验证
   25 
   26 [ ] 实测自然世界中的建议生态分布，尤其是荒村、义庄、墓地/鬼市等场景；已通过服务器检查的原版生成入口及雪原、沼泽/丛林、江河、洞窟、机关城、龙宫条件保留自然生成验收。
   27 [ ] 检查面数不超过对应基础怪；实测同屏 20 只、群体寻路和粒子负载。
   28 [ ] 两名真实玩家验证仇恨、中立受击、喂食、交易、偷窃物返还和击杀掉落不重复；不要把 FakePlayer 测试等同于真实联机验收。

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/expansion/CombatFeedback.java
    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;
    2 
    3 import com.dynasty.Dynasty;
    4 import com.dynasty.DynastyTrinketOnHit;
    5 import com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork;
    6 import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
    7 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
    8 import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
    9 import net.minecraftforge.network.*;
   10 import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
   11 import net.minecraftforge.fml.DistExecutor;
   12 import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
   13 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
   14 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority;
   15 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingDamageEvent;
   16 import java.util.function.Supplier;
   17 
   18 /** Bounded visual-only message; nearby clients never receive camera or gameplay commands. */
   19 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID)
   20 public record CombatFeedback(int type, double x, double y, double z, double sx, double sy, double sz) {
   21     public CombatFeedback(int type,double x,double y,double z){this(type,x,y,z,x,y,z);}
   22     public static void tether(LivingEntity from,net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 end) {
   23         if(!(from.level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
   24         var start=from.getEyePosition();
   25         DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(from.getX(),from.getY(),from.getZ(),32,level.dimension())),new CombatFeedback(14,end.x,end.y,end.z,start.x,start.y,start.z));
   26     }
   27     public static final int NORMAL=0, HEAVY=1, CRITICAL=2, BLOCK=3, PERFECT=4, ARMOR=5, STAGGER=6, KNOCKDOWN=7, LAUNCH=8, IMMUNE=9, THUNDER=10, WATER=11, STAR=12, HEAL=13, FIRE_RING=15, CINNABAR=16, SMALL_THUNDER=17;
   28     public static void send(LivingEntity target, int type) {
   29         if (!(target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)) return;
   30         // Per-entity throttling also bounds AoE/proc feedback in crowded encounters.
   31         long now=level.getGameTime();
   32         var data=target.getPersistentData();
   33         if (!allowsFeedback(data, now, type)) return;
   34         data.putInt("cod4FeedbackType", type);
   35         data.putLong("cod4FeedbackTick", now);
   36         DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(() -> new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(target.getX(), target.getY(), target.getZ(), 32, level.dimension())), new CombatFeedback(type,target.getX(),target.getY()+target.getBbHeight()*.55,target.getZ()));
   37     }
   38     public static boolean allowsFeedback(net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag data,long now,int type) {
   39         return !data.contains("cod4FeedbackTick") || now-data.getLong("cod4FeedbackTick")>=3
   40                 || priority(type)>priority(data.getInt("cod4FeedbackType"));
   41     }
   42     private static int priority(int type) {
   43         return switch(type) {case IMMUNE->5;case ARMOR,PERFECT,STAGGER,KNOCKDOWN,LAUNCH->4;
   44             case THUNDER,SMALL_THUNDER,FIRE_RING,CINNABAR->3;case HEAVY,CRITICAL->2;case NORMAL->0;default->1;};
   45     }
   46     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   47     public static void hit(LivingDamageEvent e) {
   48         if(e.isCanceled() || e.getAmount()<=0 || e.getEntity().level().isClientSide || DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage()) return;
   49         if (!(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity attacker)) return;
   50         var weapon=com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.sourceWeapon(e);
   51         var key=net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(weapon.getItem());
   52         if(weapon.isEmpty()||key==null||!key.getNamespace().equals("dynasty"))return;
   53         int type=NORMAL;
   54         if(e.getSource().getDirectEntity() instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow arrow)type=arrow.isCritArrow()?CRITICAL:NORMAL;
   55         else if(attacker instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player p){
   56             // High impulse must have been added by this captured primary hit.
   57             // Existing break/stagger/immune effect application owns its own cue.
   58             // WeaponLift confirms actual displacement before sending LAUNCH, including other native lift weapons.
   59             if(com.dynasty.DynastySchoolCombat.primaryCritical(p,e.getEntity()))type=CRITICAL;
   60             else if(com.dynasty.DynastySchoolCombat.primaryCharged(p,e.getEntity())&&e.getAmount()>=Math.max(6,e.getEntity().getMaxHealth()*.08F))type=HEAVY;
   61         }else if(e.getAmount()>=Math.max(6,e.getEntity().getMaxHealth()*.08F))type=HEAVY;
   62         send(e.getEntity(),type);
   63     }
   64     public static void encode(CombatFeedback p,FriendlyByteBuf b) { b.writeVarInt(p.type);b.writeDouble(p.x);b.writeDouble(p.y);b.writeDouble(p.z);b.writeDouble(p.sx);b.writeDouble(p.sy);b.writeDouble(p.sz); }
   65     public static CombatFeedback decode(FriendlyByteBuf b) {
   66         var p=new CombatFeedback(b.readVarInt(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble());
   67         if (p.type<0 || p.type>17 || !Double.isFinite(p.x+p.y+p.z+p.sx+p.sy+p.sz) || new net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3(p.x-p.sx,p.y-p.sy,p.z-p.sz).lengthSqr()>1024) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid combat feedback");
   68         return p;
   69     }
   70     public static void handle(CombatFeedback p,Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> supplier) {
   71         var ctx=supplier.get();
   72         if(ctx.getDirection()==NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT) ctx.enqueueWork(()->DistExecutor.unsafeRunWhenOn(Dist.CLIENT,()->()->ExpansionClient.feedback(p)));
   73         ctx.setPacketHandled(true);
   74     }
   75 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/expansion/ExpansionClient.java
    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;
    2 
    3 import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
    4 import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRendererProvider;
    5 import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
    6 import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
    7 import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
    8 import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
    9 import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
   10 import net.minecraftforge.client.event.EntityRenderersEvent;
   11 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
   12 import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
   13 import software.bernie.geckolib.model.GeoModel;
   14 import software.bernie.geckolib.renderer.GeoEntityRenderer;
   15 
   16 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid="dynasty",bus=Mod.EventBusSubscriber.Bus.MOD,value=Dist.CLIENT)
   17 public final class ExpansionClient {
   18     @SubscribeEvent public static void renderers(EntityRenderersEvent.RegisterRenderers event) {
   19         SecondaryMobs.TYPES.values().forEach(t->event.registerEntityRenderer(t.get(),Renderer::new));
   20         event.registerEntityRenderer(SecondaryMobs.PROJECTILE.get(),SecondaryProjectileRenderer::new);
   21         event.registerEntityRenderer(SecondaryMobs.SHRIMP.get(),Renderer::new);
   22     }
   23     @SubscribeEvent public static void setup(net.minecraftforge.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent event) {
   24         event.enqueueWork(()-> {
   25             for(var item:java.util.List.of(ExpansionContent.REPEATING,ExpansionContent.SIEGE)) {
   26                 net.minecraft.client.renderer.item.ItemProperties.register(item.get(),new ResourceLocation("pull"),(s,l,e,seed)->e!=null && e.getUseItem()==s && !net.minecraft.world.item.CrossbowItem.isCharged(s)?Math.min(1F,(s.getUseDuration()-e.getUseItemRemainingTicks())/(float)net.minecraft.world.item.CrossbowItem.getChargeDuration(s)):0F);
   27                 net.minecraft.client.renderer.item.ItemProperties.register(item.get(),new ResourceLocation("pulling"),(s,l,e,seed)->e!=null && e.getUseItem()==s && e.isUsingItem() && !net.minecraft.world.item.CrossbowItem.isCharged(s)?1F:0F);
   28                 net.minecraft.client.renderer.item.ItemProperties.register(item.get(),new ResourceLocation("charged"),(s,l,e,seed)->net.minecraft.world.item.CrossbowItem.isCharged(s)?1:0);
   29             }
   30         });
   31     }
   32     private static final class Model extends GeoModel<SecondaryMob> {
   33         private static final java.util.Set<String> DEDICATED_ATLASES=java.util.Set.of(
   34                 "river_imp","tree_spirit","shrimp_soldier","carp_spirit","snail_maiden","lantern_ghost","stone_sprite",
   35                 "crab_soldier","corpse_beetle","venom_scorpion","clockwork_rat","gray_wolf","wild_boar","golden_leopard","red_fox",
   36                 "gray_falcon","jingwei_bird","wooden_magpie","bat_demon","giant_python","stone_worm","locust_swarm",
   37                 "drowning_ghost","paper_money_ghost","wandering_spirit","bandit_thug","night_watchman","herb_picker",
   38                 "famished_refugee","swindler","paper_cut_child");
   39         @Override public ResourceLocation getModelResource(SecondaryMob m) {return new ResourceLocation("dynasty","geo/secondary/"+m.spec.id()+".geo.json");}
   40         @Override public ResourceLocation getAnimationResource(SecondaryMob m) {return new ResourceLocation("dynasty","animations/secondary/"+m.spec.id()+".animation.json");}
   41         @Override public ResourceLocation getTextureResource(SecondaryMob m) {
   42             if(DEDICATED_ATLASES.contains(m.spec.id()))
   43                 return new ResourceLocation("dynasty","textures/entity/secondary/"+m.spec.id()+".png");
   44             String atlas=switch(m.spec.family()) {case "human"->m.spec.id().equals("bandit_thug")?"assassin":"minister";case "water"->"merfolk";case "fly"->m.spec.id().equals("wooden_magpie")?"terracotta_warrior":"phoenix";case "ghost"->"soul_soldier";default->m.spec.id().equals("red_fox")?"nine_tailed_fox":"nian_beast";};
   45             return new ResourceLocation("dynasty","textures/entity/"+atlas+".png");
   46         }
   47     }
   48     private static final class Renderer extends GeoEntityRenderer<SecondaryMob> {
   49         Renderer(EntityRendererProvider.Context ctx) {super(ctx,new Model());shadowRadius=.45F;}
   50         @Override public net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType getRenderType(SecondaryMob mob,ResourceLocation texture,net.minecraft.client.renderer.MultiBufferSource buffers,float partial){
   51             return mob.spec.family().equals("ghost")?net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType.entityTranslucent(texture):super.getRenderType(mob,texture,buffers,partial);
   52         }
   53         @Override public software.bernie.geckolib.core.object.Color getRenderColor(SecondaryMob mob,float partial,int light){
   54             return mob.spec.id().equals("wandering_spirit")?software.bernie.geckolib.core.object.Color.ofRGBA(190,217,230,155):super.getRenderColor(mob,partial,light);
   55         }
   56     }
   57     private static long budgetTick=-1;private static int emitted;
   58     public static void feedback(CombatFeedback p) {
   59         var level=Minecraft.getInstance().level;if(level==null)return;
   60         if(level.getGameTime()!=budgetTick){budgetTick=level.getGameTime();emitted=0;}
   61         if(emitted>=128)return;
   62         if(p.type()==14) {
   63             int count=Math.min(32,128-emitted);emitted+=count;
   64             for(int i=0;i<count;i++){double f=(double)i/Math.max(1,count-1);level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.CRIT,p.sx()+(p.x()-p.sx())*f,p.sy()+(p.y()-p.sy())*f,p.sz()+(p.z()-p.sz())*f,0,0,0);}
   65             return;
   66         }
   67         if(p.type()==CombatFeedback.FIRE_RING || p.type()==CombatFeedback.CINNABAR || p.type()==CombatFeedback.SMALL_THUNDER) {
   68             int count=Math.min(40,128-emitted);emitted+=count;
   69             for(int i=0;i<count;i++) {
   70                 double a=i*Math.PI*2/Math.max(1,count);
   71                 if(p.type()==CombatFeedback.SMALL_THUNDER) {
   72                     double t=i/(double)Math.max(1,count-1),jitter=Math.sin(i*2.4)*.16;
   73                     level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK,p.x()+jitter,p.y()+2.5-t*2.5,p.z()-jitter,0,-.08,0);
   74                 } else {
   75                     boolean fire=p.type()==CombatFeedback.FIRE_RING;
   76                     net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleOptions particle=fire?ParticleTypes.FLAME:
   77                         new net.minecraft.core.particles.DustParticleOptions(new org.joml.Vector3f(.85F,.1F,.06F),1.3F);
   78                     double radius=fire?2.8:1.5;
   79                     level.addParticle(particle,p.x()+Math.cos(a)*radius,p.y()-.4,p.z()+Math.sin(a)*radius,Math.cos(a)*.12,.08,Math.sin(a)*.12);
   80                 }
   81             }
   82             level.playLocalSound(p.x(),p.y(),p.z(),p.type()==CombatFeedback.SMALL_THUNDER?SoundEvents.LIGHTNING_BOLT_IMPACT:SoundEvents.FIRECHARGE_USE,SoundSource.PLAYERS,.35F,1.4F,false);
   83             return;
   84         }
   85         var type=switch(p.type()) {case CombatFeedback.THUNDER->ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK;case CombatFeedback.WATER->ParticleTypes.BUBBLE;case CombatFeedback.HEAL->ParticleTypes.HAPPY_VILLAGER;case CombatFeedback.CRITICAL,CombatFeedback.STAR,CombatFeedback.PERFECT->ParticleTypes.CRIT;case CombatFeedback.ARMOR->ParticleTypes.ASH;default->ParticleTypes.ENCHANTED_HIT;};
   86         int count=p.type()==CombatFeedback.STAR?7:p.type()==CombatFeedback.NORMAL?4:12;
   87         count=Math.min(count,128-emitted);emitted+=count;
   88         for(int i=0;i<count;i++){double a=i*Math.PI*2/count;double r=p.type()==CombatFeedback.HEAVY?1.3:.55;level.addParticle(type,p.x()+Math.cos(a)*r,p.y(),p.z()+Math.sin(a)*r,Math.cos(a)*.03,.025,Math.sin(a)*.03);}
   89         level.playLocalSound(p.x(),p.y(),p.z(),p.type()==CombatFeedback.BLOCK||p.type()==CombatFeedback.PERFECT?SoundEvents.SHIELD_BLOCK:p.type()==CombatFeedback.HEAVY?SoundEvents.PLAYER_ATTACK_STRONG:SoundEvents.PLAYER_ATTACK_CRIT,SoundSource.PLAYERS,.25F,p.type()==CombatFeedback.PERFECT?1.5F:1F,false);
   90     }
   91     // EquipmentFeedback owns entity-bound VFX#4 and late-tracker replay from the firing snapshot.
   92     private ExpansionClient() { }
   93 }

    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;
    2 
    3 import com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3VisualPacket;
    4 import com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx;
    5 import com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork;
    6 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
    7 import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
    8 import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
    9 import net.minecraft.world.entity.MobType;
   10 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.*;
   11 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
   12 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.*;
   13 import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
   14 import net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor;
   15 import java.util.List;
   16 
   17 /** Uses COD3's bounded entity-bound renderer; effects and damage remain owned by the server. */
   18 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid="dynasty")
   19 public final class EffectPresentation {
   20     public static List<MobEffect> effects(){return List.of(ExpansionEffects.YIN.get(),ExpansionEffects.YANG.get(),ExpansionEffects.SHA.get(),ExpansionEffects.THUNDER.get(),ExpansionEffects.SOUL.get(),ExpansionEffects.FROST.get(),ExpansionEffects.BREAK.get(),ExpansionEffects.STAGGER.get());}
   21     public static Cod3VisualPacket packet(LivingEntity target,int code,boolean clear){
   22         var effect=target.getEffect(effects().get(code-1));
   23         int duration=clear?1:effect==null?1:effect.isInfiniteDuration()?20:Math.max(1,Math.min(20,effect.getDuration()));
   24         return new Cod3VisualPacket(target.level().dimension().location().toString(),14,target.getId(),target.getUUID().getLeastSignificantBits()+code,
   25                 target.level().getGameTime(),duration,1,target.position(),target.getLookAngle(),"status_"+(clear?"clear_":"")+code,
   26                 effect==null?0:Math.min(2,effect.getAmplifier()),effects().get(code-1).getColor());
   27     }
   28     private static void send(LivingEntity target,int code,boolean clear){
   29         if(!(target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
   30         DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(target.getX(),target.getY(),target.getZ(),32,level.dimension())),packet(target,code,clear));
   31     }
   32     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(LivingEvent.LivingTickEvent e){
   33         var target=e.getEntity();if(target.level().isClientSide)return;
   34         var data=target.getPersistentData();if(target.tickCount%20!=0&&!data.getBoolean("cod4StatusDirty"))return;
   35         data.remove("cod4StatusDirty");
   36         var effects=effects();for(int i=0;i<effects.size();i++)if(target.hasEffect(effects.get(i)))send(target,i+1,false);
   37     }
   38     @SubscribeEvent public static void added(MobEffectEvent.Added e){
   39         if(!e.getEntity().level().isClientSide&&effects().contains(e.getEffectInstance().getEffect()))e.getEntity().getPersistentData().putBoolean("cod4StatusDirty",true);
   40     }
   41     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST) public static void removed(MobEffectEvent.Remove e){
   42         if(e.isCanceled()||e.getEffectInstance()==null)return;clear(e.getEntity(),e.getEffect());
   43     }
   44     @SubscribeEvent public static void expired(MobEffectEvent.Expired e){if(e.getEffectInstance()!=null)clear(e.getEntity(),e.getEffectInstance().getEffect());}
   45     private static void clear(LivingEntity target,MobEffect effect){
   46         if(target.level().isClientSide)return;int index=effects().indexOf(effect);if(index<0)return;
   47         send(target,index+1,true);
   48         if(effect==ExpansionEffects.SHA.get())Cod3Vfx.send((ServerLevel)target.level(),14,target.position(),target.getLookAngle(),8,.08,0xE7C866);
   49     }
   50     @SubscribeEvent public static void tracking(PlayerEvent.StartTracking e){
   51         if(!(e.getEntity() instanceof net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer player)||!(e.getTarget() instanceof LivingEntity target))return;
   52         var effects=effects();for(int i=0;i<effects.size();i++)if(target.hasEffect(effects.get(i)))
   53             DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.PLAYER.with(()->player),packet(target,i+1,false));
   54     }
   55     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOW) public static void yangDamage(LivingHurtEvent e){
   56         if(e.isCanceled()||e.getAmount()<=0||e.getEntity().level().isClientSide||e.getEntity().getMobType()!=MobType.UNDEAD
   57                 ||!(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity attacker)||com.dynasty.DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage())return;
   58         var yang=attacker.getEffect(ExpansionEffects.YANG.get());if(yang==null)return;
   59         e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*(1+.08F*(Math.min(2,yang.getAmplifier())+1)));
   60     }
   61     private EffectPresentation(){}
   62 }

    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;

    3 import com.dynasty.DynastyEffects;
    4 import com.dynasty.DynastyBalance;
    5 import net.minecraft.world.effect.*;
    6 import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
    7 import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.*;
    8 import net.minecraftforge.registries.RegistryObject;
    9 import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
   10 import java.util.UUID;
   11 
   12 /** Effects share the existing registry; all stacking and damage are server-authoritative. */
   13 @net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid="dynasty")
   14 public final class ExpansionEffects {
   15     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> YIN = effect("yin_qi", false, 0x556A83, Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, -.06);
   16     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> YANG = effect("yang_qi", true, 0xE7C866, Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE, .04);
   17     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> SHA = effect("sha_qi", false, 0x652D72, Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE, -.12);
   18     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> THUNDER = effect("thunder_mark", false, 0x8FE3F3, null, 0);
   19     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> SOUL = effect("soul_burn", false, 0x46BCDA, null, 0);
   20     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> FROST = effect("frost_vein", false, 0xBCE9F5, Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, -.18);
   21     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> BREAK = effect("armor_break", false, 0xB78664, Attributes.ARMOR, -.20);
   22     public static final RegistryObject<MobEffect> STAGGER = effect("stagger", false, 0xAAA09B, Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, -.65);
   23     public static void bootstrap() { }
   24     /** Existing environmental relief, before native effect-map iteration; no parallel status ledger. */
   25     @net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent(priority=net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority.HIGHEST)
   26     public static void environment(net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingEvent.LivingTickEvent event){
   27         var target=event.getEntity();
   28         if(target.level().isClientSide||!target.isAlive()||target.tickCount%20!=0||!target.hasEffect(FROST.get())||!target.isInWater())return;
   29         if(GeothermalWater.warm(target.level(),target.blockPosition()))target.removeEffect(FROST.get());
   30     }
   31     private static RegistryObject<MobEffect> effect(String id, boolean good, int color, Attribute attr, double amount) {
   32         return DynastyEffects.EFFECTS.register(id, () -> new MobEffect(good ? MobEffectCategory.BENEFICIAL : MobEffectCategory.HARMFUL, color) {
   33             { if (attr != null) addAttributeModifier(attr, UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes(("dynasty:"+id).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString(), amount, AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL); }
   34             @Override public boolean isDurationEffectTick(int duration, int amplifier) { return duration % 20 == 0; }
   35             @Override public void applyEffectTick(LivingEntity target, int amplifier) {
   36                 if (target.level().isClientSide) return;
   37                 if (id.equals("soul_burn")) target.hurt(target.damageSources().magic(), target.hasEffect(MobEffects.FIRE_RESISTANCE) ? 1 : 2);
   38                 if (id.equals("yin_qi")) target.hurt(target.damageSources().magic(), amplifier + 1);
   39                 if (id.equals("frost_vein") && target.isOnFire()) target.removeEffect(this);
   40             }
   41         });
   42     }
   43     public static boolean boss(LivingEntity target) {
   44         return target instanceof com.dynasty.ritual.ZhenyuanSovereign || target instanceof com.dynasty.DynastyBossCombat.BarHolder || !target.canChangeDimensions() || DynastyBalance.isBossOrBeast(target.getType());
   45     }
   46     public static void apply(LivingEntity target, RegistryObject<MobEffect> type, int duration) {
   47         if (target.level().isClientSide) return;
   48         // Reject before opposite-Qi neutralisation: an immune hit must not consume a valid buff.
   49         if (QiConstitution.immune(target, type.get())) { CombatFeedback.send(target, CombatFeedback.IMMUNE); return; }
   50         if ((type == SHA || type == STAGGER) && boss(target)) { CombatFeedback.send(target, CombatFeedback.IMMUNE); return; }
   51         if (type == YIN && target.hasEffect(YANG.get())) { target.removeEffect(YANG.get());if(target instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player p)EquipmentBehaviors.neutralisedQi(p);return; }
   52         if (type == YANG && target.hasEffect(YIN.get())) { target.removeEffect(YIN.get());if(target instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player p)EquipmentBehaviors.neutralisedQi(p);return; }
   53         var old = target.getEffect(type.get());
   54         int amp = (type == YIN || type == YANG || type == THUNDER) && old != null ? Math.min(2, old.getAmplifier()+1) : 0;
   55         if (type == THUNDER && amp == 2) {
   56             target.removeEffect(type.get());
   57             target.hurt(target.damageSources().magic(), 12);
   58             CombatFeedback.send(target, CombatFeedback.THUNDER);
   59             if(target.level() instanceof net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel level)
   60                 com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.send(level,31,target.position(),target.getLookAngle(),12,.45,0x8FE3F3);
   61             return;
   62         }
   63         target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(type.get(), type == BREAK && boss(target) ? duration/2 : duration, amp));
   64     }
   65     private ExpansionEffects() { }
   66 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/expansion/QiConstitution.java
    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;
    2 
    3 import com.dynasty.Dynasty;
    4 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
    5 import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffect;
    6 import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
    7 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingEquipmentChangeEvent;
    8 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingEvent;
    9 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.MobEffectEvent;
   10 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.Event;
   11 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority;
   12 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
   13 import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
   14 
   15 /** A live affinity of the existing day/night sets, not another saved status system. */
   16 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = Dynasty.MODID)
   17 public final class QiConstitution {
   18     public static boolean immune(LivingEntity target, MobEffect effect) {
   19         if (!(target instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return false;
   20         if (effect == ExpansionEffects.YIN.get())
   21             return player.level().isDay() && EquipmentBehaviors.pieces(player, "tiangang") == 4;
   22         if (effect == ExpansionEffects.YANG.get())
   23             return !player.level().isDay() && EquipmentBehaviors.pieces(player, "disha") == 4;
   24         return false;
   25     }
   26 
   27     /** Covers native /effect, potions and other callers as well as ExpansionEffects.apply. */
   28     @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
   29     public static void applicable(MobEffectEvent.Applicable event) {
   30         if (immune(event.getEntity(), event.getEffectInstance().getEffect())) {
   31             event.setResult(Event.Result.DENY);
   32             CombatFeedback.send(event.getEntity(), CombatFeedback.IMMUNE);
   33         }
   34     }
   35 
   36     public static void cleanse(ServerPlayer player) {
   37         if (player.hasEffect(ExpansionEffects.YIN.get()) && immune(player, ExpansionEffects.YIN.get()))
   38             player.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.YIN.get());
   39         if (player.hasEffect(ExpansionEffects.YANG.get()) && immune(player, ExpansionEffects.YANG.get()))
   40             player.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.YANG.get());
   41     }
   42 
   43     @SubscribeEvent
   44     public static void equipment(LivingEquipmentChangeEvent event) {
   45         if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) cleanse(player);
   46     }
   47 
   48     /** Before native effect ticking: also handles login, dawn/dusk and a dimension change. */
   49     @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
   50     public static void tick(LivingEvent.LivingTickEvent event) {
   51         if (event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) cleanse(player);
   52     }
   53 
   54     private QiConstitution() { }
   55 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/expansion/EquipmentBehaviors.java
    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;

    3 import com.dynasty.*;
    4 import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
    5 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;

   10 import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

   12 import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
   13 import net.minecraftforge.event.TickEvent;
   14 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.*;

   20 
   21 /** Adds conditional behavior to existing armor/trinkets; IDs, base stats, and school combat stay intact. */
   22 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID)
   23 public final class EquipmentBehaviors {
   24     private static final Map<Player,Map<MobEffect,MobEffectInstance>> SHORTEN = new WeakHashMap<>();
   25     private static boolean shortening;
   26     private static final String KEY="dynastyCod4";
   27     public static int pieces(Player p,String set) {
   28         return (int)DynastySetBonus.wornArmorIds(p).stream().filter(id->id.equals(set+"_helmet") || id.equals(set+"_chestplate") || id.equals(set+"_leggings") || id.equals(set+"_boots")).count();
   29     }
   30     public static CompoundTag saved(Player p) {
   31         CompoundTag persisted=p.getPersistentData().getCompound(Player.PERSISTED_NBT_TAG);
   32         if(!persisted.contains(KEY))persisted.put(KEY,new CompoundTag());
   33         p.getPersistentData().put(Player.PERSISTED_NBT_TAG,persisted);return persisted.getCompound(KEY);
   34     }
   35     public static long now(Player p) {return p.level().getGameTime();}
   36     public static boolean ready(Player p,String key,int ticks) {
   37         var n=saved(p);long time=now(p);if(n.contains(key) && time<n.getLong(key))return false;
   38         n.putLong(key,time+ticks);return true;
   39     }
   40     private static boolean has(Player p,String id) {return DynastyTrinkets.activeIds(p).contains(id);}
   41     private static void attribute(Player p,String key,Attribute attr,double amount,AttributeModifier.Operation op) {
   42         var a=p.getAttribute(attr);if(a==null)return;
   43         UUID id=UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes(("dynasty:cod4/"+key).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
   44         var old=a.getModifier(id);if(old!=null && old.getAmount()==amount)return;
   45         a.removeModifier(id);if(amount!=0)a.addTransientModifier(new AttributeModifier(id,key,amount,op));
   46     }
   47     public static void refresh(Player p) {
   48         boolean day=p.level().isDay(),wet=p.isInWater(),full=p.getHealth()>=p.getMaxHealth();
   49         var n=p.getPersistentData();boolean still=n.getInt("cod4Still")>=40;
   50         double speed=pieces(p,"cloth")>=2 && p.getFoodData().getFoodLevel()==20?.05:0;
   51         if(pieces(p,"leather")>=2 && p.isShiftKeyDown())speed+=.15;
   52         if(pieces(p,"bamboo")>=3 && p.isSprinting() && p.level().getBiome(p.blockPosition()).is(net.minecraft.world.level.biome.Biomes.BAMBOO_JUNGLE))speed+=.10;
   53         if(pieces(p,"jade")>=4 && full)speed+=.08;
   54         if(pieces(p,"sea_silk")>=2 && wet)speed+=.20;
   55         if(pieces(p,"beidou")>=2)speed+=.08;
   56         if(pieces(p,"phoenix")>=3 && now(p)<n.getLong("cod4PhoenixSpeed"))speed+=.15;
   57         attribute(p,"speed",Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED,speed,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   58         double armor=pieces(p,"general")>=3 && still?12:0;
   59         if(pieces(p,"dragon_scale")>=4 && wet)armor+=10;
   60         if(pieces(p,"bronze")>=3 && now(p)<n.getLong("cod4BronzeGuard"))armor+=6;
   61         attribute(p,"armor",Attributes.ARMOR,armor,AttributeModifier.Operation.ADDITION);
   62         attribute(p,"resistance",Attributes.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE,pieces(p,"dark_iron")>=2?1:pieces(p,"general")>=2?.2:0,AttributeModifier.Operation.ADDITION);
   63         double luck=pieces(p,"qilin")>=2?1:pieces(p,"ziwei")>=2?3:0;
   64         if(has(p,"qilin_horn_charm"))luck+=2;
   65         if(has(p,"jade_bi_disc") && !has(p,"moon_pendant") && !day)luck+=1;
   66         if(has(p,"moon_pendant") && !day && p.level().getMoonPhase()==0)luck+=2;
   67         attribute(p,"swim",net.minecraftforge.common.ForgeMod.SWIM_SPEED.get(),pieces(p,"sea_silk")>=3?1:0,AttributeModifier.Operation.ADDITION);
   68         attribute(p,"luck",Attributes.LUCK,luck,AttributeModifier.Operation.ADDITION);
   69         attribute(p,"school_sword_speed",Attributes.ATTACK_SPEED,Set.of("liuyun_sword","chengying_sword").contains(id(p.getMainHandItem())) && DynastySchoolProgression.equippedSynergy(p,"sword")?.08:0,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   70         double all=pieces(p,"hongmeng")>=2?.06:pieces(p,"xuantian")>=2?.05:pieces(p,"taiyi")>=2?.04:0;
   71         long time=p.level().getDayTime()%24000;
   72         if(pieces(p,"tiangang")>=4 && time>=5500 && time<=6500 || pieces(p,"disha")>=4 && time>=17500 && time<=18500)all+=.08;
   73         if(pieces(p,"hunyuan")>=2)all+=day?.04:.02;
   74         attribute(p,"all_attack",Attributes.ATTACK_DAMAGE,all,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   75         attribute(p,"all_armor",Attributes.ARMOR,all,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   76         attribute(p,"all_health",Attributes.MAX_HEALTH,all,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   77         attribute(p,"all_speed",Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED,all,AttributeModifier.Operation.MULTIPLY_TOTAL);
   78         ownedEffect(p,MobEffects.NIGHT_VISION,pieces(p,"draco_king")>=2 || pieces(p,"zhuque")>=2);
   79         ownedEffect(p,MobEffects.DOLPHINS_GRACE,pieces(p,"sea_silk")>=4 && wet);
   80         skyJump(p);
   81         if(pieces(p,"beidou")<4) {n.remove("cod4StarIFrame");n.remove("cod4ContinuousSteps");}
   82         if(pieces(p,"xuanwu")<4)n.remove("cod4Shell");
   83         if(!counterActive(p))clearCounter(p);
   84         // These are equipment grants, not cooldowns. Losing their threshold revokes
   85         // the window so putting the set back on cannot revive an earlier proc.
   86         if(pieces(p,"cloth")<3)n.remove("cod4Rested");
   87         if(pieces(p,"bronze")<3)n.remove("cod4BronzeGuard");
   88         if(pieces(p,"phoenix")<3)n.remove("cod4PhoenixSpeed");
   89         if(pieces(p,"ziwei")<3)n.remove("cod4Crit");
   90         if(pieces(p,"hunyuan")<3)n.remove("cod4BalanceUntil");
   91         if(pieces(p,"hunyuan")<4)n.remove("cod4HunyuanQiUntil");
   92         if(pieces(p,"qinglong")<4)n.remove("cod4QinglongQiUntil");
   93         if(pieces(p,"dark_iron")<3 && pieces(p,"hunyuan")<4 && now(p)>=n.getLong("cod4QiUntil"))n.remove("cod4Rage");
   94         if(p.getHealth()>p.getMaxHealth())p.setHealth(p.getMaxHealth());
   95     }
   96     private static void skyJump(Player p) {
   97         var data=p.getPersistentData();var current=p.getEffect(MobEffects.JUMP);
   98         boolean enabled=pieces(p,"sky")>=4;
   99         if(enabled && current!=null && current.getAmplifier()<1) {
  100             // Vanilla would hide a weaker potion under our effect; save it before upgrading so
  101             // unequipping can restore its remaining duration instead of removing somebody else's buff.
  102             var backup=new CompoundTag();current.save(backup);data.put("cod4JumpBackup",backup);
  103             data.putLong("cod4JumpBackupUntil",current.isInfiniteDuration()?Long.MAX_VALUE:now(p)+current.getDuration());
  104             p.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.JUMP,30,1,true,false));
  105             data.putBoolean("cod4Own_minecraft:jump_boost",true);
  106         }
  107         ownedEffect(p,MobEffects.JUMP,enabled,1);
  108         if(!enabled && data.contains("cod4JumpBackup")) {
  109             var backup=MobEffectInstance.load(data.getCompound("cod4JumpBackup"));
  110             long remaining=data.getLong("cod4JumpBackupUntil")-now(p);
  111             if(backup!=null && remaining>0 && !p.hasEffect(MobEffects.JUMP))
  112                 p.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.JUMP,backup.isInfiniteDuration()?-1:(int)Math.min(Integer.MAX_VALUE,remaining),backup.getAmplifier(),backup.isAmbient(),backup.isVisible(),backup.showIcon()));
  113             data.remove("cod4JumpBackup");data.remove("cod4JumpBackupUntil");
  114         }
  115     }
  116     private static void ownedEffect(Player p,MobEffect effect,boolean enabled) {
  117         ownedEffect(p,effect,enabled,0);
  118     }
  119     private static void ownedEffect(Player p,MobEffect effect,boolean enabled,int amplifier) {
  120         String key="cod4Own_"+net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries.MOB_EFFECTS.getKey(effect);
  121         var current=p.getEffect(effect);boolean ours=current!=null && current.isAmbient() && !current.isVisible() && current.getAmplifier()==amplifier && current.getDuration()<=30;
  122         if(enabled && (current==null || ours)) {p.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(effect,30,amplifier,true,false));p.getPersistentData().putBoolean(key,true);}
  123         else if(!enabled && p.getPersistentData().getBoolean(key)) {if(ours)p.removeEffect(effect);p.getPersistentData().remove(key);}
  124     }
  125     @SubscribeEvent public static void equipment(LivingEquipmentChangeEvent e) {if(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p)refresh(p);}
  126     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(TickEvent.PlayerTickEvent e) {
  127         if(e.phase!=TickEvent.Phase.END || !(e.player instanceof ServerPlayer p))return;
  128         var n=p.getPersistentData();double moved=p.position().distanceToSqr(new net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3(n.getDouble("cod4X"),n.getDouble("cod4Y"),n.getDouble("cod4Z")));
  129         n.putInt("cod4Still",moved<.0001?Math.min(80,n.getInt("cod4Still")+1):0);
  130         n.putDouble("cod4X",p.getX());n.putDouble("cod4Y",p.getY());n.putDouble("cod4Z",p.getZ());
  131         if(moved>.16 && moved<16) {
  132             n.putLong("cod4Moved",now(p));
  133         }
  134         advanceStarStep(p,moved);
  135         if(pieces(p,"cloth")>=4 && p.isSprinting() && moved>0 && moved<4) {
  136             var food=p.getFoodData();food.setExhaustion(Math.max(0,food.getExhaustionLevel()-(float)Math.sqrt(moved)*.1F));
  137         }
  138         Map<MobEffect,MobEffectInstance> pending=SHORTEN.remove(p);
  139         if(pending!=null) {
  140             shortening=true;
  141             try {for(var entry:pending.entrySet()) {
  142                 var current=p.getEffect(entry.getKey());var desired=entry.getValue();
  143                 if(current!=null && current.getAmplifier()==desired.getAmplifier() && current.getDuration()>desired.getDuration()) {p.removeEffect(entry.getKey());p.addEffect(desired);}
  144             }} finally {shortening=false;}
  145         }
  146         if(pieces(p,"sky")>=2 && p.getDeltaMovement().y<-.15 && !p.onGround())p.setDeltaMovement(p.getDeltaMovement().multiply(1,.65,1));
  147         if(pieces(p,"dragon_scale")>=2 && p.isInWater() || pieces(p,"draco_king")>=2 && p.isInWater())p.setAirSupply(p.getMaxAirSupply());
  148         if(pieces(p,"cinnabar")>=4 || now(p)<n.getLong("cod4Antidote"))p.removeEffect(MobEffects.POISON);
  149         if(p.tickCount%20!=0)return;
  150         refresh(p);
  151         if(pieces(p,"brocade")>=3 && now(p)-n.getLong("cod4LastCombat")>200)p.heal(1);
  152         if(pieces(p,"sea_silk")>=4 && p.isInWater())p.heal(1);
  153         if(has(p,"jade_cicada") && p.getHealth()<p.getMaxHealth()*.3 && ready(p,"cicada",3600)){p.heal(p.getMaxHealth()*.08F);CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.HEAL);com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"jade_cicada");}
  154         purifyCinnabar(p);
  155         if(pieces(p,"xuantian")>=4)for(var mob:p.level().getEntitiesOfClass(Mob.class,p.getBoundingBox().inflate(4),m->m.getTarget()==p && !ExpansionEffects.boss(m)))mob.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.WEAKNESS,30,0));
  156         if(pieces(p,"draco_king")>=3) SummonedGuard.maintain(p,pieces(p,"draco_king")>=4?2:1);
  157         taiyiGlow(p);
  158         releaseHunyuanQi(p);
  159         if(has(p,"sea_pearl") && p.isInWater() && p.tickCount%60==0)CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.WATER);
  160     }
  161     @SubscribeEvent public static void footsteps(net.minecraftforge.event.PlayLevelSoundEvent.AtEntity e) {
  162         if(e.getEntity() instanceof Player p && p.isShiftKeyDown() && pieces(p,"leather")>=4 && e.getSound()!=null && e.getSound().value().getLocation().getPath().endsWith(".step"))e.setCanceled(true);
  163     }
  164     @SubscribeEvent public static void enchanting(net.minecraftforge.event.entity.player.PlayerXpEvent.LevelChange e) {
  165         var p=e.getEntity();if(p.level().isClientSide || e.getLevels()>=0 || pieces(p,"jade")<2 || !(p.containerMenu instanceof net.minecraft.world.inventory.EnchantmentMenu))return;
  166         var saved=saved(p);int credit=saved.getInt("enchantCredit")-e.getLevels();int refund=credit/10;
  167         saved.putInt("enchantCredit",credit%10);e.setLevels(Math.min(0,e.getLevels()+refund));
  168     }
  169     @SubscribeEvent public static void wake(PlayerWakeUpEvent e) {if(!e.getEntity().level().isClientSide && pieces(e.getEntity(),"cloth")>=3)e.getEntity().getPersistentData().putLong("cod4Rested",now(e.getEntity())+1200);}
  170     @SubscribeEvent public static void fall(LivingFallEvent e) {
  171         if(!(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p))return;
  172         if(pieces(p,"sky")>=4)e.setCanceled(true);else if(pieces(p,"bamboo")>=2)e.setDistance(Math.max(0,e.getDistance()-1));
  173     }
  174     /** Ten consecutive sprint displacement ticks; teleports, stopping and unequipping reset the sequence. */
  175     public static void advanceStarStep(Player p,double moved) {
  176         var n=p.getPersistentData();
  177         int steps=pieces(p,"beidou")>=4 && p.isSprinting() && !p.isPassenger() && moved>.01 && moved<4
  178                 ? Math.min(10,n.getInt("cod4ContinuousSteps")+1) : 0;
  179         n.putInt("cod4ContinuousSteps",steps);
  180         if(steps==10 && ready(p,"starStep",200)) {
  181             n.putInt("cod4ContinuousSteps",0);n.putLong("cod4StarIFrame",now(p)+4);
  182             CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.STAR);
  183             com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.actor(p,"beidou_stride",14,1,0xaacdff);
  184         }
  185     }
  186     public static boolean auspiciousGuard(Player p,float roll) {
  187         return pieces(p,"qilin")>=3 && roll<.15F;
  188     }
  189     public static boolean taiyiGlow(ServerPlayer p){
  190         if(pieces(p,"taiyi")<4||p.experienceLevel<30||!ready(p,"taiyiGlow",6000))return false;
  191         p.heal(p.getMaxHealth()*.05F);CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.HEAL);
  192         com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.send(p.serverLevel(),14,p.position(),p.getLookAngle(),20,.1,0xE7C866);
  193         return true;
  194     }
  195     public static void phoenixIgnited(Player p) {
  196         p.getPersistentData().putLong("cod4PhoenixAttack",now(p)+60);
  197         CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.FIRE_RING);
  198         com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.actor(p,"phoenix_embers",16,.6,0xff8050);
  199     }
  200     public static boolean phoenixAttackActive(Player p) {
  201         return p.isOnFire() || now(p)<p.getPersistentData().getLong("cod4PhoenixAttack");
  202     }
  203     /** Balance is a recent neutralisation of the two existing effects, never merely Yang alone. */
  204     public static boolean balanced(Player p){
  205         return pieces(p,"hunyuan")>=3&&now(p)<p.getPersistentData().getLong("cod4BalanceUntil")
  206                 &&!p.hasEffect(ExpansionEffects.YIN.get())&&!p.hasEffect(ExpansionEffects.YANG.get());
  207     }
  208     public static void neutralisedQi(Player p){
  209         if(pieces(p,"hunyuan")>=3)p.getPersistentData().putLong("cod4BalanceUntil",now(p)+100);
  210     }
  211     /** All three grants feed the original combo resource. Equipment grants require the equipment. */
  212     public static boolean qiActive(Player p){
  213         var n=p.getPersistentData();long time=now(p);
  214         return time<n.getLong("cod4QiUntil")||pieces(p,"qinglong")>=4&&time<n.getLong("cod4QinglongQiUntil")
  215                 ||pieces(p,"hunyuan")>=4&&time<n.getLong("cod4HunyuanQiUntil");
  216     }
  217     public static boolean releaseHunyuanQi(Player p){
  218         var n=p.getPersistentData();
  219         if(pieces(p,"hunyuan")<4||n.getInt("cod4Rage")<5||!ready(p,"hunyuanQi",200))return false;
  220         n.putInt("cod4Rage",0);n.putLong("cod4HunyuanQiUntil",now(p)+100);
  221         CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.PERFECT);return true;
  222     }
  223     public static boolean comboWave(ServerPlayer p,LivingEntity target,int combo){
  224         if(combo<5||!(pieces(p,"qinglong")>=3||has(p,"dragon_pearl")||strengthenedWave(p))||!ready(p,"comboWave",60))return false;
  225         var packet=new com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3VisualPacket(p.level().dimension().location().toString(),7,target.getId(),
  226                 p.getRandom().nextLong(),now(p),20,strengthenedWave(p)?1.3:1,target.position(),p.getLookAngle(),"qinglong_combo_wave",0,0x54CCB8);
  227         com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(
  228                 target.getX(),target.getY(),target.getZ(),32,p.level().dimension())),packet);
  229         if(strengthenedWave(p) && !ExpansionEffects.boss(target))
  230             target.knockback(.35,p.getX()-target.getX(),p.getZ()-target.getZ());
  231         if(strengthenedWave(p) && id(p.getMainHandItem()).equals("thunder_spear"))ExpansionEffects.apply(target,ExpansionEffects.THUNDER,60);
  232         if(pieces(p,"qinglong")>=4){p.heal(p.getMaxHealth()*.02F);p.getPersistentData().putLong("cod4QinglongQiUntil",now(p)+60);}
  233         return true;
  234     }
  235     public static void smallThunder(Player p,LivingEntity target) {
  236         if(!ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,target))return;
  237         // Add to this real hit once, without a lightning entity that can burn terrain or hit bystanders.
  238         CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.SMALL_THUNDER);
  239     }
  240     public static void purifyCinnabar(ServerPlayer p) {
  241         if(pieces(p,"cinnabar")<3 || !p.hasEffect(ExpansionEffects.SHA.get()))return;
  242         p.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.SHA.get());
  243         for(var ally:p.level().getEntitiesOfClass(LivingEntity.class,p.getBoundingBox().inflate(3),
  244                 e->e==p || e.isAlliedTo(p) || e instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.TamableAnimal pet && p.getUUID().equals(pet.getOwnerUUID())))
  245             if(ally.distanceToSqr(p)<=9)ally.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.SHA.get());
  246         CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.CINNABAR);
  247     }
  248     @SubscribeEvent public static void effect(MobEffectEvent.Added e) {
  249         if(!shortening && e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p && pieces(p,"cinnabar")>=2 && !e.getEffectInstance().getEffect().isBeneficial()) {
  250             var effect=e.getEffectInstance();if(effect.isInfiniteDuration())return;
  251             SHORTEN.computeIfAbsent(p,k->new HashMap<>()).put(effect.getEffect(),new MobEffectInstance(effect.getEffect(),Math.max(1,effect.getDuration()*3/4),effect.getAmplifier(),effect.isAmbient(),effect.isVisible(),effect.showIcon()));
  252         }
  253     }
  254     @SubscribeEvent public static void reflect(net.minecraftforge.event.entity.ProjectileImpactEvent e) {
  255         if(e.getProjectile().level().isClientSide || !(e.getProjectile() instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow arrow) || !(e.getRayTraceResult() instanceof net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult hit) || !(hit.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p))return;
  256         if(pieces(p,"bamboo")>=4 && p.getRandom().nextFloat()<.05F) {e.setCanceled(true);arrow.setOwner(p);arrow.setDeltaMovement(arrow.getDeltaMovement().scale(-.8));arrow.pickup=net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow.Pickup.DISALLOWED;arrow.hurtMarked=true;CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.BLOCK);}
  257     }
  258     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST) public static void block(ShieldBlockEvent e) {
  259         if(e.isCanceled()||e.getBlockedDamage()<=0||!(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p))return;
  260         var n=p.getPersistentData();if(pieces(p,"bronze")>=3)n.putLong("cod4BronzeGuard",now(p)+60);
  261         boolean perfect=perfectGuard(p);
  262         if(perfect)p.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.STAGGER.get());
  263         String blocking=id(p.getUseItem());
  264         if(Set.of("zhenyue_blade","beichen_spear").contains(blocking) && DynastySchoolProgression.equippedSynergy(p,"guard"))
  265             e.setBlockedDamage(Math.min(e.getOriginalBlockedDamage(),e.getBlockedDamage()+e.getOriginalBlockedDamage()*.08F));
  266         if(blocking.equals("xuanwu_blade") && symbolEquipped(p,"xuanwu"))
  267             e.setBlockedDamage(Math.min(e.getOriginalBlockedDamage(),e.getBlockedDamage()+e.getOriginalBlockedDamage()*.1F));
  268         if(perfect && pieces(p,"xuanwu")>=2) {
  269             // Run after the school's partial block. Raise a 50% weapon block to 60%
  270             // of the original hit; an ordinary shield's full block remains full.
  271             e.setBlockedDamage(Math.min(e.getOriginalBlockedDamage(),Math.max(e.getBlockedDamage(),e.getOriginalBlockedDamage()*.6F)));
  272             openCounter(p,"xuanwu");
  273             if(pieces(p,"xuanwu")>=4)n.putBoolean("cod4Shell",true);
  274         }
  275         if(has(p,"heart_mirror") && !has(p,"bronze_mirror") && e.getDamageSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity attacker && ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,attacker)) {
  276             DynastyTrinketOnHit.syntheticDamage(()->attacker.hurt(p.damageSources().thorns(p),Math.min(20,e.getBlockedDamage()*.1F)));
  277         }
  278         CombatFeedback.send(p,perfect?CombatFeedback.PERFECT:CombatFeedback.BLOCK);
  279     }
  280     /** Vanilla raises a block after five ticks; the next six active ticks are the 0.3s window. */
  281     public static boolean perfectGuard(Player p){
  282         boolean paired=p.isUsingItem()&&p.getUseItem().is(ExpansionContent.DUCK.get())&&pairedAxes(p);
  283         return (p.isBlocking()||paired)&&p.getTicksUsingItem()>=5&&p.getTicksUsingItem()<11;
  284     }
  285     private static boolean pairedAxes(Player p){
  286         return p.getMainHandItem().is(ExpansionContent.DUCK.get())&&p.getOffhandItem().is(ExpansionContent.DUCK.get());
  287     }
  288     private static void openCounter(Player p,String source){
  289         p.getPersistentData().putLong("cod4Counter",now(p)+60);
  290         p.getPersistentData().putString("cod4CounterSource",source);
  291     }
  292     private static boolean counterActive(Player p){
  293         var n=p.getPersistentData();
  294         if(now(p)>=n.getLong("cod4Counter"))return false;
  295         return switch(n.getString("cod4CounterSource")){
  296             case "xuanwu"->pieces(p,"xuanwu")>=2;
  297             case "duck"->pairedAxes(p);
  298             default->false; // An old unowned three-second window cannot acquire a new source.
  299         };
  300     }
  301     private static void clearCounter(Player p){
  302         p.getPersistentData().remove("cod4Counter");p.getPersistentData().remove("cod4CounterSource");
  303     }
  304     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOW) public static void attack(LivingAttackEvent e) {
  305         if(!(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p) || e.getAmount()<=0)return;
  306         if(pieces(p,"beidou")>=4 && now(p)<p.getPersistentData().getLong("cod4StarIFrame") && !e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.BYPASSES_INVULNERABILITY))e.setCanceled(true);
  307         if(e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.IS_FIRE) && (pieces(p,"phoenix")>=2 || pieces(p,"zhuque")>=2 || has(p,"phoenix_feather_charm"))) {
  308             if(has(p,"phoenix_ring"))phoenixIgnited(p);
  309             if(has(p,"phoenix_feather_charm"))com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"phoenix_feather_charm");
  310             e.setCanceled(true);p.clearFire();if(pieces(p,"phoenix")>=3)p.getPersistentData().putLong("cod4PhoenixSpeed",now(p)+60);
  311             if(pieces(p,"phoenix")>=4 && ready(p,"fireRing",80)) {
  312                 CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.FIRE_RING);
  313                 com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.actor(p,"phoenix_embers",16,.6,0xff8050);
  314                 for(var mob:p.level().getEntitiesOfClass(LivingEntity.class,p.getBoundingBox().inflate(3),m->ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,m) && m.distanceToSqr(p)<=9))mob.setSecondsOnFire(3);
  315             }
  316         }
  317     }
  318     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOW) public static void hurt(LivingHurtEvent e) {
  319         if(e.getEntity().level().isClientSide || e.getAmount()<=0 || e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.BYPASSES_INVULNERABILITY) || DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage())return;
  320         if(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p) {
  321             var n=p.getPersistentData();n.putLong("cod4LastCombat",now(p));
  322             if((pieces(p,"dark_iron")>=3 && n.getInt("cod4Still")>=40) || pieces(p,"hunyuan")>=4)n.putInt("cod4Rage",Math.min(5,n.getInt("cod4Rage")+1));
  323             if(has(p,"jade_pendant") && !has(p,"heart_mirror") && p.getHealth()<p.getMaxHealth()*.4F){e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*.88F);com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"jade_pendant");}
  324             if(auspiciousGuard(p,p.getRandom().nextFloat())) {
  325                 e.setAmount(0);e.setCanceled(true);CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.PERFECT);return;
  326             }
  327             if(pieces(p,"bronze")>=2 && p.getRandom().nextFloat()<.1F)
  328                 p.level().playSound(null,p.blockPosition(),net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents.SHIELD_BLOCK,net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.PLAYERS,.7F,.65F);
  329             if(e.getAmount()>0 && pieces(p,"qilin")>=4 && p.getHealth()<p.getMaxHealth()*.25 && ready(p,"qilinShield",6000)){e.setAmount(0);CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.PERFECT);}
  330             if(pieces(p,"bronze")>=4 && e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof com.dynasty.entity.ImperialSoldier)e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*.8F);
  331             if(has(p,"fox_tail_charm") && p.getRandom().nextFloat()<.1 && e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof Mob mob && !ExpansionEffects.boss(mob)){mob.setTarget(null);mob.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN,40,1));com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"fox_tail_charm");}
  332             if(p.isUsingItem() && p.getUseItem().is(ExpansionContent.DUCK.get()) && pairedAxes(p) && !e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.BYPASSES_SHIELD)) {
  333                 if(e.getSource().getSourcePosition()!=null && p.getLookAngle().dot(e.getSource().getSourcePosition().subtract(p.position()).normalize())>0) {e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*.35F);openCounter(p,"duck");boolean perfect=perfectGuard(p);if(perfect)p.removeEffect(ExpansionEffects.STAGGER.get());CombatFeedback.send(p,perfect?CombatFeedback.PERFECT:CombatFeedback.BLOCK);com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.weapon(p,"duck_guard",null);}
  334             }
  335         }
  336         if(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof SecondaryMob mob && (mob.spec.id().equals("golden_leopard") || mob.spec.id().equals("gray_falcon")) && e.getEntity().getHealth()>=e.getEntity().getMaxHealth()*.9F)e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*1.5F);
  337         if(!(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p) || !ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,e.getEntity()))return;
  338         var n=p.getPersistentData();n.putLong("cod4LastCombat",now(p));float bonus=0;boolean day=p.level().isDay();
  339         if(pieces(p,"cloth")>=3 && now(p)<n.getLong("cod4Rested"))bonus+=.08F;
  340         if(pieces(p,"leather")>=3 && p.isShiftKeyDown() && ready(p,"sneak",120))bonus+=.3F;
  341         if(pieces(p,"general")>=4 && n.getInt("cod4Still")>=40)bonus+=.15F;
  342         if(pieces(p,"silver")>=2 && e.getEntity().getMobType()==MobType.UNDEAD)bonus+=.15F;
  343         if(pieces(p,"silver")>=3 && !day)bonus+=.1F;
  344         if(pieces(p,"jade")>=3 && p.getHealth()>=p.getMaxHealth())bonus+=.08F;
  345         if(pieces(p,"dragon_scale")>=3 && p.isInWater())bonus+=.12F;
  346         if(pieces(p,"sky")>=3 && !p.onGround())bonus+=.2F;
  347         if(pieces(p,"xuantian")>=3 && ExpansionEffects.boss(e.getEntity()))bonus+=.1F;
  348         if(pieces(p,"zhuque")>=3 && e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.IS_FIRE))bonus+=.15F;
  349         if(pieces(p,"tiangang")>=2 && day || pieces(p,"disha")>=2 && !day)bonus+=.1F;
  350         if(pieces(p,"beidou")>=3 && now(p)-n.getLong("cod4Moved")<60)bonus+=.1F;
  351         if(pieces(p,"hongmeng")>=3 && e.getEntity() instanceof com.dynasty.ritual.ZhenyuanSovereign)bonus+=.15F;
  352         if(pieces(p,"dark_iron")>=4 && n.getInt("cod4Rage")>=5){n.putInt("cod4Rage",0);bonus+=.25F;CombatFeedback.send(e.getEntity(),CombatFeedback.HEAVY);}
  353         if(counterActive(p)){boolean duck=n.getString("cod4CounterSource").equals("duck");clearCounter(p);bonus+=pieces(p,"xuanwu")>=3?.2F:.1F;if(duck)com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.weapon(p,"duck_counter",null);}
  354         if(pieces(p,"baihu")>=3 && p.isSprinting() && ready(p,"sprintStrike",60)){bonus+=.4F;if(pieces(p,"baihu")>=4)ExpansionEffects.apply(e.getEntity(),ExpansionEffects.STAGGER,40);}
  355         if(pieces(p,"baihu")>=2 && !ExpansionEffects.boss(e.getEntity()))e.getEntity().knockback(.5,p.getX()-e.getEntity().getX(),p.getZ()-e.getEntity().getZ());
  356         if(balanced(p))bonus+=.1F;
  357         if(pieces(p,"ziwei")>=3 && p.getRandom().nextFloat()<.1){bonus+=.5F;n.putLong("cod4Crit",now(p)+60);CombatFeedback.send(e.getEntity(),CombatFeedback.CRITICAL);}
  358         if(pieces(p,"ziwei")>=4 && now(p)<n.getLong("cod4Crit"))bonus+=.15F;
  359         if(has(p,"dragon_scale_charm") && net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(e.getEntity().getType()).getPath().contains("dragon")){bonus+=.15F;com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"dragon_scale_charm");}
  360         if(has(p,"phoenix_ring") && phoenixAttackActive(p))bonus+=.15F;
  361         float thunderDamage=0;
  362         if(has(p,"storm_charm") && p.level().isThundering() && p.getRandom().nextFloat()<.1F) {
  363             smallThunder(p,e.getEntity());thunderDamage=4;
  364         }
  365         if(e.getSource().getDirectEntity()==p && e.getSource().is(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK) && DynastySchoolCombat.chargedPrimary(p,e.getEntity())) {
  366             int combo=DynastyTrinketOnHit.advanceCombo(p,e.getEntity());
  367             if(pieces(p,"qinglong")>=2)bonus+=.06F*Math.min(3,combo);
  368             if(comboWave(p,e.getEntity(),combo))bonus+=strengthenedWave(p)?.18F:.12F;
  369         }
  370         var used=DynastySchoolCombat.firingWeapon(e.getSource(),p.getMainHandItem());
  371         if(e.getSource().getDirectEntity() instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow && Set.of("zhuxing_bow","fengling_bow").contains(id(used)) && DynastySchoolProgression.equippedSynergy(p,"archer")){bonus+=.08F;CombatFeedback.send(e.getEntity(),CombatFeedback.STAR);}
  372         e.setAmount(e.getAmount()*(1+bonus+seriesBonus(p,e.getEntity(),used))+twinBonus(p,used)+thunderDamage);
  373     }
  374     public static boolean symbolEquipped(Player p,String symbol) {
  375         return pieces(p,symbol)>0 || DynastyTrinkets.activeIds(p).stream().anyMatch(s->s.startsWith(symbol+"_"));
  376     }
  377     public static boolean strengthenedWave(Player p) {
  378         return Set.of("qinglong_dao","thunder_spear").contains(id(p.getMainHandItem())) && symbolEquipped(p,"qinglong");
  379     }
  380     private static float seriesBonus(Player p,LivingEntity target,ItemStack used) {
  381         String main=id(used);
  382         float bonus=0;
  383         String element=main.startsWith("xuanwu")?"xuanwu":main.startsWith("zhuque")?"zhuque":main.startsWith("qinglong")||main.equals("thunder_spear")?"qinglong":main.startsWith("baihu")?"baihu":"";
  384         if(!element.isEmpty() && symbolEquipped(p,element))bonus+=.1F;
  385         return bonus+revengeBonus(p,target,main);
  386     }
  387     /** Canonical drop/gift provenance, with no extra weapon-use gate or second reward flag. */
  388     public static float revengeBonus(Player p,LivingEntity target,String weapon){
  389         if(p.isAlliedTo(target)||target.isAlliedTo(p))return 0;
  390         var n=p.getPersistentData();boolean eligible=false;
  391         String source=switch(weapon){case "dragon_spear"->"undead_first_emperor";case "yitian_sword"->"rebel_general";
  392             case "qinggang_sword"->"eunuch_mastermind";case "dragon_slayer"->"dragon_emperor";default->"";};
  393         if(!source.isEmpty())eligible=n.getBoolean("dynasty_firstkill_"+source)&&faction(target,source);
  394         if(weapon.equals("sunbow"))eligible=n.getBoolean("dynasty_gift_sunbow")&&faction(target,"phoenix");
  395         if(weapon.equals("supreme_sword"))eligible=(DynastyStats.getRank(p)>=17||n.getBoolean("dynasty_gift_supreme_sword"))&&faction(target,"court");
  396         if(weapon.equals("seven_star_saber")&&n.getInt("dynasty_boss_kinds")>=5){
  397             var defeated=new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(n.getString("dynasty_boss_kinds_list").split(",")));
  398             for(String boss:List.of("dragon_emperor","rebel_general","eunuch_mastermind","undead_first_emperor","nine_heaven_general","dragon_king"))
  399                 if((defeated.contains(boss)||n.getBoolean("dynasty_firstkill_"+boss))&&faction(target,boss)){eligible=true;break;}
  400         }
  401         return eligible?.15F:0;
  402     }
  403     private static boolean faction(LivingEntity target,String source){
  404         var id=net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(target.getType());if(id==null)return false;
  405         if(source.equals("undead_first_emperor")&&target.getMobType()==MobType.UNDEAD)return true;
  406         if(!id.getNamespace().equals("dynasty"))return false;String name=id.getPath();
  407         return switch(source){
  408             case "undead_first_emperor"->name.equals(source);
  409             case "dragon_king"->Set.of("dragon_king","merfolk","shrimp_soldier","crab_soldier","river_imp","carp_spirit").contains(name);
  410             case "rebel_general"->name.startsWith("rebel")||name.equals("bandit_thug");
  411             case "nine_heaven_general"->Set.of("thunder_envoy","nine_heaven_general").contains(name);
  412             case "phoenix"->name.equals("phoenix");
  413             case "court"->Set.of("rebel_general","rebel_soldier","bandit_thug","assassin","eunuch_mastermind").contains(name);
  414             default->Set.of(source,"royal_guard","jade_guard").contains(name);
  415         };
  416     }
  417     public static float twinBonus(Player p,ItemStack used) {
  418         Set<String> imperial=Set.of("qilin_war_axe","taiyi_sword","baihu_glaive","thunder_spear","ziwei_saber","zhuque_bow");
  419         String main=id(used),off=id(p.getOffhandItem());
  420         return imperial.contains(main) && imperial.contains(off) && !main.equals(off)?(float)DynastyBalance.weaponBonus(main)*.1F:0;
  421     }
  422     public static int cooldown(Player p,int base) {return pieces(p,"taiyi")>=3?Math.max(1,base*9/10):base;}
  423     public static String id(ItemStack s) {var id=net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(s.getItem());return id!=null && id.getNamespace().equals("dynasty")?id.getPath():"";}
  424     public static void symbolHit(ServerPlayer p,LivingEntity target,ItemStack used) {
  425         if(!ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,target))return;
  426         String weapon=id(used);
  427         if(Set.of("zhuque_fan","zhuque_bow").contains(weapon) && symbolEquipped(p,"zhuque") && !target.fireImmune() && ready(p,"symbolFire",40)) {
  428             target.setSecondsOnFire(4);CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.FIRE_RING);
  429         }
  430         if(weapon.equals("baihu_glaive") && symbolEquipped(p,"baihu") && ready(p,"symbolBreak",80)) {
  431             ExpansionEffects.apply(target,ExpansionEffects.BREAK,60);CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.ARMOR);
  432         }
  433     }
  434     @SubscribeEvent public static void damage(LivingDamageEvent e) {
  435         if(e.getEntity().level().isClientSide || e.getAmount()<=0)return;
  436         if(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer attacker && !DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage()
  437                 && (e.getSource().getDirectEntity()==attacker || e.getSource().getDirectEntity() instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow))
  438             symbolHit(attacker,e.getEntity(),DynastySchoolCombat.firingWeapon(e.getSource(),attacker.getMainHandItem()));
  439         if(e.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p && pieces(p,"silver")>=4 && !p.level().isDay() && ExpansionWeapons.enemy(p,e.getEntity()))p.heal(e.getAmount()*.05F);
  440         if(!(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p) || e.getSource().is(DamageTypeTags.BYPASSES_INVULNERABILITY) || e.getAmount()<p.getHealth())return;
  441         boolean shell=pieces(p,"xuanwu")>=4 && p.getPersistentData().getBoolean("cod4Shell");
  442         String key=pieces(p,"hongmeng")>=4?"hongmengLife":pieces(p,"zhuque")>=4?"zhuqueLife":has(p,"phoenix_feather_charm")?"phoenixLife":shell?"xuanwuLife":"";
  443         if(!key.isEmpty() && ready(p,"lastStand",6000)) {if(key.equals("phoenixLife"))com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.accessory(p,"phoenix_feather_charm");e.setAmount(0);p.setHealth(Math.max(1,p.getMaxHealth()*.15F));p.getPersistentData().remove("cod4Shell");CombatFeedback.send(p,CombatFeedback.HEAL);}
  444     }
  445     @SubscribeEvent public static void kill(LivingDeathEvent e) {
  446         if(e.getEntity().getKillCredit() instanceof ServerPlayer p && (pieces(p,"tiangang")>=3 && p.level().isDay() || pieces(p,"disha")>=3 && !p.level().isDay()))p.heal(p.getMaxHealth()*.02F);
  447     }
  448     private EquipmentBehaviors() { }
  449 }

   24     public static final class SpecialArrow extends ArrowItem {
   25         private final String kind;
   26         SpecialArrow(String kind) { super(new Properties());this.kind=kind; }
   27         @Override public AbstractArrow createArrow(Level level,ItemStack stack,LivingEntity shooter) {
   28             var arrow=super.createArrow(level,stack,shooter);
   29             arrow.getPersistentData().putString("cod4Ammo",kind);
   30             if(kind.equals("pierce_arrow") || kind.equals("heavy_bolt")) arrow.setPierceLevel((byte)1);
   31             if(kind.equals("poison_arrow") && arrow instanceof Arrow a) a.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.POISON,100,0));
   32             return arrow;
   33         }

   35     public static final class BurstCrossbow extends CrossbowItem {
   36         private final boolean siege;
   37         BurstCrossbow(boolean siege) { super(new Properties().durability(siege?2200:1600));this.siege=siege; }
   38         @Override public Predicate<ItemStack> getAllSupportedProjectiles() {
   39             return s -> s.getItem() instanceof ArrowItem && (!isBolt(s) || s.is(ExpansionContent.AMMO.get(siege?"heavy_bolt":"repeating_bolt").get()));
   40         }
   41         @Override public Predicate<ItemStack> getSupportedHeldProjectiles() { return getAllSupportedProjectiles(); }
   42         @Override public int getUseDuration(ItemStack stack) { return 72000; }
   43         @Override public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level,Player p,InteractionHand hand) {
   44             ItemStack stack=p.getItemInHand(hand);
   45             if(!isCharged(stack) && p.getProjectile(stack).isEmpty() && !p.getAbilities().instabuild) return InteractionResultHolder.fail(stack);
   46             p.startUsingItem(hand);return InteractionResultHolder.consume(stack);
   47         }
   48         @Override public void releaseUsing(ItemStack stack,Level level,LivingEntity user,int remaining) {
   49             if(level.isClientSide || !(user instanceof Player p) || isCharged(stack) || getUseDuration(stack)-remaining < CrossbowItem.getChargeDuration(stack)) return;
   50             ListTag magazine=new ListTag();
   51             for(int n=0;n<(siege?1:3);n++) {
   52                 ItemStack ammo=p.getProjectile(stack);
   53                 if(ammo.isEmpty()) { if(!p.getAbilities().instabuild) break; ammo=new ItemStack(Items.ARROW); }
   54                 ItemStack shot=ammo.copy();shot.setCount(1);magazine.add(shot.save(new CompoundTag()));
   55                 if(!p.getAbilities().instabuild) ammo.shrink(1);
   56             }
   57             if(!magazine.isEmpty()) { stack.getOrCreateTag().put("cod4Magazine",magazine);setCharged(stack,true);level.playSound(null,p.blockPosition(),SoundEvents.CROSSBOW_LOADING_END,SoundSource.PLAYERS,1,1); }
   58         }
   59         @Override public void onUseTick(Level level,LivingEntity user,ItemStack stack,int remaining) {
   60             if(level.isClientSide || !(user instanceof Player p) || !isCharged(stack) || (getUseDuration(stack)-remaining)%6!=0) return;
   61             ListTag magazine=stack.getOrCreateTag().getList("cod4Magazine",Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
   62             if(magazine.isEmpty()) { setCharged(stack,false);p.stopUsingItem();return; }
   63             ItemStack ammo=ItemStack.of(magazine.getCompound(0));magazine.remove(0);
   64             if(!(ammo.getItem() instanceof ArrowItem item)) { setCharged(stack,false);p.stopUsingItem();return; }
   65             AbstractArrow arrow=item.createArrow(level,ammo,p);
   66             arrow.shootFromRotation(p,p.getXRot(),p.getYRot(),0,3.15F,siege?.2F:1F);
   67             arrow.setBaseDamage((siege?900:260)/3.15);arrow.setShotFromCrossbow(true);
   68             arrow.setPierceLevel((byte)Math.max(arrow.getPierceLevel(),siege?2:0));
   69             arrow.pickup=p.getAbilities().instabuild?AbstractArrow.Pickup.CREATIVE_ONLY:AbstractArrow.Pickup.ALLOWED;
   70             arrow.getPersistentData().putBoolean("cod4Siege",siege);
   71             arrow.getPersistentData().put("cod4FiringWeapon",stack.save(new CompoundTag()));
   72             level.addFreshEntity(arrow);
   73             com.dynasty.cod3.EquipmentFeedback.weapon(p,siege?"siege_crossbow":"repeating_crossbow",null);
   74             if(siege) { p.push(-p.getLookAngle().x*.25,0,-p.getLookAngle().z*.25);p.hurtMarked=true; }
   75             stack.hurtAndBreak(1,p,e->e.broadcastBreakEvent(p.getUsedItemHand()));
   76             level.playSound(null,p.blockPosition(),SoundEvents.CROSSBOW_SHOOT,SoundSource.PLAYERS,1,siege?.7F:1.2F);
   77             if(magazine.isEmpty()) { setCharged(stack,false);p.stopUsingItem(); }
   78         }
   79     }
   80     public static boolean isBolt(ItemStack s) {
   81         return s.is(ExpansionContent.AMMO.get("heavy_bolt").get()) || s.is(ExpansionContent.AMMO.get("repeating_bolt").get());
   82     }

  107             } else return InteractionResultHolder.pass(stack);
  108             p.getCooldowns().addCooldown(this,EquipmentBehaviors.cooldown(p,40));stack.hurtAndBreak(1,p,e->e.broadcastBreakEvent(hand));
  109             return InteractionResultHolder.success(stack);
