  131                 com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3Vfx.send(server,27,p.position(),p.getLookAngle(),12,.25);
  132             }
  133             p.getCooldowns().addCooldown(this,EquipmentBehaviors.cooldown(p,80));stack.hurtAndBreak(3,p,e->e.broadcastBreakEvent(p.getUsedItemHand()));
  134         }
  135     }
  136     @SubscribeEvent public static void ammoHit(LivingHurtEvent e) {
  137         if(e.getEntity().level().isClientSide || !(e.getSource().getDirectEntity() instanceof AbstractArrow arrow)) return;

    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;

    3 import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
    4 import net.minecraft.network.syncher.*;
    5 import net.minecraft.world.effect.*;
    6 import net.minecraft.world.entity.*;
    7 import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ThrowableItemProjectile;
    8 import net.minecraft.world.item.*;
    9 import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
   10 import net.minecraft.world.phys.*;
   11 
   12 /** One real, finite projectile per ranged action. Visuals never apply a second hit. */
   13 public final class SecondaryProjectile extends ThrowableItemProjectile implements net.minecraftforge.entity.IEntityAdditionalSpawnData {
   14     public static final int SEED=0, LIGHT=1, WATER=2, STONE=3;
   15     private static final EntityDataAccessor<Integer> KIND=SynchedEntityData.defineId(SecondaryProjectile.class,EntityDataSerializers.INT);
   16     public SecondaryProjectile(EntityType<? extends SecondaryProjectile> type,Level level){super(type,level);}
   17     @Override protected void defineSynchedData(){super.defineSynchedData();entityData.define(KIND,SEED);}
   18     public int kind(){return entityData.get(KIND);}
   19     public void kind(int kind){entityData.set(KIND,Math.max(SEED,Math.min(STONE,kind)));}
   20     @Override public net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet<net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientGamePacketListener> getAddEntityPacket(){return net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkHooks.getEntitySpawningPacket(this);}
   21     @Override public void writeSpawnData(net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf b){b.writeVarInt(kind());b.writeInt(getOwner()==null?-1:getOwner().getId());}
   22     @Override public void readSpawnData(net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf b){kind(b.readVarInt());int owner=b.readInt();if(owner>=0)setOwner(level().getEntity(owner));}
   23     @Override protected Item getDefaultItem(){return switch(kind()){case LIGHT->Items.GLOWSTONE_DUST;case WATER->Items.PRISMARINE_CRYSTALS;case STONE->Items.FLINT;default->Items.WHEAT_SEEDS;};}
   24     @Override protected float getGravity(){return kind()==STONE?.035F:kind()==SEED?.008F:0;}
   25     @Override protected boolean canHitEntity(Entity e){return super.canHitEntity(e)&&!(getOwner() instanceof LivingEntity owner&&owner.isAlliedTo(e));}
   26     @Override protected void onHitEntity(EntityHitResult hit){
   27         super.onHitEntity(hit);
   28         if(level().isClientSide||!(getOwner() instanceof SecondaryMob owner)||!(hit.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity target)
   29                 ||!target.isAlive()||owner.isAlliedTo(target))return;
   30         if(!target.hurt(damageSources().mobAttack(owner),(float)Math.max(1,owner.spec.damage()/6)))return;
   31         switch(kind()){
   32             case LIGHT->{target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.BLINDNESS,40));ExpansionEffects.apply(target,ExpansionEffects.SOUL,60);CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.STAR);}
   33             case SEED->{ExpansionEffects.apply(target,ExpansionEffects.STAGGER,30);CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.STAGGER);
   34                 if(!ExpansionEffects.boss(target)&&target.level() instanceof net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel server){
   35                     var packet=new com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3VisualPacket(server.dimension().location().toString(),14,target.getId(),target.getUUID().getLeastSignificantBits(),
   36                         server.getGameTime(),30,1,target.position(),target.getLookAngle(),"secondary_roots",0,0x749644);
   37                     com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->
   38                         new net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(target.getX(),target.getY(),target.getZ(),32,server.dimension())),packet);
   39                 }}
   40             case WATER->{owner.beginWaterDrag(target);CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.WATER);}
   41             case STONE->CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.HEAVY);
   42             default->{}
   43         }
   44     }
   45     @Override protected void onHit(HitResult hit){super.onHit(hit);if(!level().isClientSide)discard();}
   46     @Override public void tick(){if(!level().isClientSide&&tickCount>=40){discard();return;}super.tick();}
   47     @Override public void addAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag n){super.addAdditionalSaveData(n);n.putInt("Cod4ProjectileKind",kind());n.putInt("Cod4ProjectileAge",tickCount);}
   48     @Override public void readAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag n){super.readAdditionalSaveData(n);kind(n.getInt("Cod4ProjectileKind"));tickCount=Math.max(0,Math.min(40,n.getInt("Cod4ProjectileAge")));}
   49 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/expansion/SecondaryCombatActions.java
    1 package com.dynasty.expansion;
    2 
    3 import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
    4 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
    5 import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
    6 import net.minecraft.world.effect.*;
    7 import net.minecraft.world.entity.*;
    8 import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
    9 import java.util.*;
   10 
   11 /** Finite phases of the existing mob AI. Damage is owned by one actual contact, never the animation. */
   12 final class SecondaryCombatActions {
   13     private final SecondaryMob mob;
   14     private String action="";
   15     private UUID targetId;
   16     private int age,holdTicks,evadeTicks;
   17     private boolean hit,pinch;
   18     private Vec3 launch=Vec3.ZERO,holdAnchor;
   19     private BlockPos water;
   20     private UUID heldId;
   21     private long holdReady;
   22     SecondaryCombatActions(SecondaryMob mob){this.mob=mob;}
   23     boolean active(){return !action.isEmpty()||evadeTicks>0;}
   24     String phase(){return action;}
   25     boolean begin(LivingEntity target){
   26         if(active()||target==null||!target.isAlive()||mob.isAlliedTo(target)||!mob.hasLineOfSight(target)||mob.distanceToSqr(target)>100)return false;
   27         if(mob.spec.id().equals("venom_scorpion")&&mob.distanceToSqr(target)>4)return false;
   28         action=switch(mob.spec.id()){
   29             case "red_fox"->"pounce";case "golden_leopard"->"stalk";
   30             case "gray_falcon","bat_demon","wooden_magpie"->"circle";
   31             case "carp_spirit"->mob.isInWater()?"return_water":"";
   32             case "crab_soldier"->"side_charge";
   33             case "venom_scorpion"->pinch?"sting":"pinch";
   34             case "corpse_beetle","stone_worm"->mob.level().getBlockState(mob.blockPosition().below()).isSolid()?"burrow":"";
   35             default->"";
   36         };
   37         if(action.isEmpty())return false;
   38         targetId=target.getUUID();age=0;hit=false;launch=Vec3.ZERO;mob.getNavigation().stop();
   39         if(mob.spec.id().equals("carp_spirit"))water=mob.blockPosition();
   40         mob.skillAnimation(action);
   41         if(mob.spec.id().equals("wooden_magpie")){
   42             mob.playSound(SoundEvents.NOTE_BLOCK_BIT.get(),.6F,1.3F);
   43             for(var other:mob.level().getEntitiesOfClass(SecondaryMob.class,mob.getBoundingBox().inflate(12),m->m.spec.id().equals("wooden_magpie")&&m!=mob))other.setTarget(target);
   44             visual(target,"secondary_alarm",21,0xBD965B,16);
   45         }
   46         return true;
   47     }
   48     void tick(){
   49         tickHold();
   50         if(evadeTicks>0){evadeTicks--;mob.getNavigation().stop();mob.skillAnimation("evade");return;}
   51         if(action.isEmpty()||!(mob.level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
   52         var entity=level.getEntity(targetId);
   53         if(!(entity instanceof LivingEntity target)||!target.isAlive()||mob.isAlliedTo(target)||mob.distanceToSqr(target)>196||!mob.hasLineOfSight(target)){cancel();return;}
   54         mob.getNavigation().stop();mob.getLookControl().setLookAt(target,30,30);age++;
   55         String id=mob.spec.id();
   56         if(Set.of("gray_falcon","bat_demon","wooden_magpie").contains(id)){
   57             if(age<=20){
   58                 action="circle";double a=age*.22;
   59                 steer(target.position().add(Math.cos(a)*2.5,3.5,Math.sin(a)*2.5),.22);
   60             }else if(age<=30){
   61                 action="dive";steer(target.getBoundingBox().getCenter(),.7);contact(target);
   62             }else{
   63                 action="circle";steer(target.position().add(0,4,0),.24);
   64                 if(age>=46)cancel();
   65             }
   66         }else if(id.equals("carp_spirit")){
   67             if(age<5)action="return_water";
   68             else if(age==5){action="pounce";launch=horizontal(target).scale(.48);mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,.52,launch.z);mob.hurtMarked=true;}
   69             else if(age<=18){action="pounce";contact(target);}
   70             else{
   71                 action="return_water";
   72                 if(water==null||!level.hasChunkAt(water)||!level.getFluidState(water).is(net.minecraft.tags.FluidTags.WATER)){cancel();return;}
   73                 steer(Vec3.atCenterOf(water),.28);
   74                 if(mob.isInWater()||age>=44)cancel();
   75             }
   76         }else if(id.equals("crab_soldier")){
   77             action="side_charge";
   78             if(age==1){var f=horizontal(target);launch=new Vec3(f.z,0,-f.x).scale(.28);}
   79             if(age<=6)mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,launch.z);
   80             else if(age<=18){if(age==7)launch=horizontal(target).scale(.42);mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,launch.z);contact(target);}
   81             else cancel();
   82         }else if(id.equals("corpse_beetle")||id.equals("stone_worm")){
   83             if(age<=18){
   84                 action="burrow";
   85                 if(age==7)launch=horizontal(target).scale(.32);
   86                 if(age>=7){if(mob.horizontalCollision){cancel();return;}mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,launch.z);}
   87             }else{
   88                 action="eruption";
   89                 if(age==19){launch=horizontal(target).scale(.38);mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,.28,launch.z);mob.hurtMarked=true;CombatFeedback.send(mob,CombatFeedback.LAUNCH);}
   90                 contact(target);if(age>=30)cancel();
   91             }
   92         }else if(id.equals("venom_scorpion")){
   93             if(age<6)mob.setDeltaMovement(0,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,0);
   94             else if(age<=12){var f=horizontal(target).scale(.18);mob.setDeltaMovement(f.x,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,f.z);contact(target);}
   95             else if(age>=18)cancel();
   96         }else{
   97             int release=id.equals("golden_leopard")?12:6;
   98             if(age<release){action=id.equals("golden_leopard")?"stalk":"pounce";mob.setDeltaMovement(0,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,0);}
   99             else{
  100                 action="pounce";
  101                 if(age==release){launch=horizontal(target).scale(id.equals("golden_leopard")?.62:.48);mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,.23,launch.z);mob.hurtMarked=true;}
  102                 else if(age<release+8)mob.setDeltaMovement(launch.x,mob.getDeltaMovement().y,launch.z);
  103                 contact(target);if(age>=release+16||mob.horizontalCollision)cancel();
  104             }
  105         }
  106         if(!action.isEmpty())mob.skillAnimation(action);
  107     }
  108     private Vec3 horizontal(LivingEntity target){return target.position().subtract(mob.position()).multiply(1,0,1).normalize();}
  109     private void steer(Vec3 point,double speed){
  110         var delta=point.subtract(mob.position());
  111         mob.setDeltaMovement(delta.lengthSqr()<speed*speed?delta:delta.normalize().scale(speed));mob.hurtMarked=true;
  112     }
  113     private void contact(LivingEntity target){
  114         if(hit||!mob.getBoundingBox().inflate(.15).intersects(target.getBoundingBox())||!mob.hasLineOfSight(target))return;
  115         // Re-entry through doHurtTarget adds the mob's original poison, theft, leech, etc. once.
  116         if(mob.doHurtTarget(target)){
  117             hit=true;
  118             if(mob.spec.id().equals("bat_demon"))visual(target,"secondary_echo",26,0xB899D6,16);
  119             if(mob.spec.id().equals("golden_leopard"))CombatFeedback.send(target,CombatFeedback.ARMOR);
  120         }
  121     }
  122     void successfulMelee(LivingEntity target){
  123         switch(mob.spec.id()){
  124             case "giant_python"->{if(bind(target,32)){mob.skillAnimation("coil");visual(target,"secondary_coil",13,0x719852,32);}}
  125             case "crab_soldier"->{bind(target,18);mob.skillAnimation("pinch");}
  126             case "venom_scorpion"->{
  127                 pinch=!pinch;
  128                 if(pinch){bind(target,18);mob.skillAnimation("pinch");}else mob.skillAnimation("sting");
  129             }
  130             case "wandering_spirit"->{mob.skillAnimation("possession");visual(target,"secondary_possession",15,0x6F92A5,30);}
  131             default->{}
  132         }
  133     }
  134     private boolean bind(LivingEntity target,int duration){
  135         if(ExpansionEffects.boss(target)||mob.isAlliedTo(target)||mob.level().getGameTime()<holdReady)return false;
  136         heldId=target.getUUID();holdAnchor=target.position();holdTicks=duration;
  137         holdReady=mob.level().getGameTime()+80;
  138         // This is the existing vanilla movement effect plus bounded impulses, not input/camera control.
  139         target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN,duration,4));
  140         return true;
  141     }
  142     private void tickHold(){
  143         if(holdTicks<=0||!(mob.level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
  144         var entity=level.getEntity(heldId);
  145         if(!(entity instanceof LivingEntity target)||!target.isAlive()||ExpansionEffects.boss(target)||mob.isAlliedTo(target)
  146                 ||mob.distanceToSqr(target)>9||!mob.hasLineOfSight(target)||!target.hasEffect(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN)){holdTicks=0;return;}
  147         holdTicks--;
  148         var old=target.getDeltaMovement();var returnForce=holdAnchor.subtract(target.position()).multiply(1,0,1).scale(.18);
  149         if(returnForce.lengthSqr()>.01)returnForce=returnForce.normalize().scale(.1);
  150         target.setDeltaMovement(old.x*.1+returnForce.x,old.y,old.z*.1+returnForce.z);target.hurtMarked=true;
  151         if(mob.spec.id().equals("giant_python")){
  152             mob.skillAnimation("coil");
  153             if(holdTicks%5==0)target.setAirSupply(Math.max(0,target.getAirSupply()-8));
  154             if(holdTicks==12)target.hurt(target.damageSources().drown(),2);
  155         }
  156     }
  157     void evade(LivingEntity attacker){
  158         if(!mob.spec.id().equals("red_fox")||attacker==null||evadeTicks>0)return;
  159         var away=mob.position().subtract(attacker.position()).multiply(1,0,1).normalize();
  160         var side=new Vec3(away.z,0,-away.x).scale(.4);
  161         if(!mob.level().noCollision(mob,mob.getBoundingBox().move(side.scale(2))))side=side.scale(-1);
  162         if(!mob.level().noCollision(mob,mob.getBoundingBox().move(side.scale(2))))return;
  163         cancel();evadeTicks=6;mob.setDeltaMovement(side.x,.12,side.z);mob.hurtMarked=true;mob.skillAnimation("evade");
  164     }
  165     private static void visual(LivingEntity target,String sequence,int template,int tint,int duration){
  166         if(!(target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
  167         var packet=new com.dynasty.cod3.Cod3VisualPacket(level.dimension().location().toString(),template,target.getId(),
  168                 level.random.nextLong(),level.getGameTime(),duration,1,target.position(),target.getLookAngle(),sequence,0,tint);
  169         com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->
  170                 new net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(target.getX(),target.getY(),target.getZ(),32,level.dimension())),packet);
  171     }
  172     void cancel(){action="";targetId=null;age=0;launch=Vec3.ZERO;mob.getNavigation().stop();}
  173     void reset(){cancel();holdTicks=0;evadeTicks=0;heldId=null;holdAnchor=null;water=null;}
  174 }

    9 /** Fixed wire IDs; registration and handshake schema are identical on both physical sides. */
   10 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID, bus=Mod.EventBusSubscriber.Bus.MOD)
   11 public final class CombatFeedbackNetworkRegistration {
   12     @SubscribeEvent public static void setup(FMLCommonSetupEvent event) {

   14             DynastyNetwork.registerExtension(16, com.dynasty.expansion.CombatFeedback.class,
   15                     com.dynasty.expansion.CombatFeedback::encode, com.dynasty.expansion.CombatFeedback::decode,

   17         });
   18     }
   19     private CombatFeedbackNetworkRegistration() {}
   20 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/cod3/Cod3VisualPacket.java
    1 package com.dynasty.cod3;
    2 
    3 import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
    4 import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
    5 import net.minecraftforge.fml.DistExecutor;
    6 import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
    7 import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;
    8 import java.util.function.Supplier;
    9 
   10 /** World-space effects only: this packet has no camera, screen overlay or player-input fields. */
   11 public record Cod3VisualPacket(String dimension,int template,int entityId,long seed,long start,int duration,double scale,Vec3 origin,Vec3 direction,String sequence,int tick,int tint) {
   12     public Cod3VisualPacket(String dimension,int template,int entityId,long seed,long start,int duration,double scale,Vec3 origin,Vec3 direction,String sequence,int tick){this(dimension,template,entityId,seed,start,duration,scale,origin,direction,sequence,tick,0);}
   13     public static void encode(Cod3VisualPacket p,FriendlyByteBuf b){b.writeUtf(p.dimension,128);b.writeVarInt(p.template);b.writeInt(p.entityId);b.writeLong(p.seed);b.writeLong(p.start);b.writeVarInt(p.duration);b.writeDouble(p.scale);write(b,p.origin);write(b,p.direction);b.writeUtf(p.sequence,128);b.writeVarInt(p.tick);b.writeInt(p.tint);}
   14     private static void write(FriendlyByteBuf b,Vec3 v){b.writeDouble(v.x);b.writeDouble(v.y);b.writeDouble(v.z);}
   15     private static Vec3 read(FriendlyByteBuf b){return new Vec3(b.readDouble(),b.readDouble(),b.readDouble());}
   16     public static Cod3VisualPacket decode(FriendlyByteBuf b){return new Cod3VisualPacket(b.readUtf(128),b.readVarInt(),b.readInt(),b.readLong(),b.readLong(),b.readVarInt(),b.readDouble(),read(b),read(b),b.readUtf(128),b.readVarInt(),b.readInt());}
   17     public boolean valid(){return dimension!=null&&net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation.tryParse(dimension)!=null&&sequence!=null&&sequence.length()<=128&&tick>=0&&entityId>=-1&&origin!=null&&direction!=null&&(template!=0||!sequence.isBlank()&&tick<=duration)&&template>=0&&template<=40&&duration>0&&duration<=2400&&tint>=0&&tint<=0xffffff&&Double.isFinite(scale)&&scale>0&&scale<=32&&Double.isFinite(origin.lengthSqr())&&Double.isFinite(direction.lengthSqr());}
   18     public static void handle(Cod3VisualPacket p,Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> supplier){var c=supplier.get();if(p.valid()&&c.getDirection().getReceptionSide().isClient())c.enqueueWork(()->DistExecutor.unsafeRunWhenOn(Dist.CLIENT,()->()->{
   19         if(!com.dynasty.client.SevenStarFlashRenderer.receive(p)&&!com.dynasty.client.WeaponImpactRenderer.receive(p)
   20                 &&!com.dynasty.client.TangWindRenderer.receive(p)&&!com.dynasty.client.ArmorCrackRenderer.receive(p)
   21                 &&!com.dynasty.client.DragonDecalRenderer.receive(p)&&!com.dynasty.client.WeaponLiftRenderer.receive(p)&&!com.dynasty.client.FangtianSweepRenderer.receive(p)&&!com.dynasty.client.FluteWaveRenderer.receive(p))com.dynasty.client.Cod3VfxRenderer.receive(p);
   22     }));c.setPacketHandled(true);}
   23 }

FILE src/main/java/com/dynasty/cod3/WeaponImpactVisual.java
    1 package com.dynasty.cod3;
    2 
    3 import com.dynasty.network.DynastyNetwork;
    4 import com.dynasty.Dynasty;
    5 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
    6 import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
    7 import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
    8 import net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor;
    9 import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingDamageEvent;
   10 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
   11 import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority;
   12 import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
   13 import net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries;
   14 import java.util.*;
   15 
   16 /** Small material-specific hit geometry. Never applies damage, effects or impulses. */
   17 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID)
   18 public final class WeaponImpactVisual {
   19     public static final String PREFIX="equipment_impact_";
   20     public enum Shape { CHIP, SPARK }
   21     public record Burst(int onset,int count,double rotation,double speed,double length,double width,int life) {}
   22     public record Style(String weapon,int code,int duration,int color,Shape shape,List<Burst> bursts) {}
   23     public record Triangle(Vec3 a,Vec3 b,Vec3 c,int color,double alpha) {}
   24     private static final Map<String,Style> STYLES=Map.of(
   25         "mu_mao",new Style("mu_mao",0,8,0xB48C59,Shape.CHIP,List.of(new Burst(0,4,.2,.08,.14,.035,8))),
   26         "shi_ge",new Style("shi_ge",1,10,0x909794,Shape.CHIP,List.of(new Burst(0,3,.9,.065,.085,.05,10))),
   27         "tong_dao",new Style("tong_dao",2,8,0xD5A46D,Shape.SPARK,List.of(new Burst(0,5,.2,.10,.16,.02,8))),
   28         "tie_jian",new Style("tie_jian",3,7,0xA9B8BD,Shape.SPARK,List.of(new Burst(0,4,.7,.13,.21,.018,7))),
   29         "qinggang_sword",new Style("qinggang_sword",4,9,0x86DFC8,Shape.SPARK,List.of(
   30             new Burst(0,3,.25,.12,.22,.02,5),new Burst(3,3,1.25,.15,.27,.02,6))),
   31         "gilded_mace",new Style("gilded_mace",5,12,0xE5BE72,Shape.SPARK,List.of(
   32             new Burst(0,2,0,.035,.08,.035,3),new Burst(2,4,.45,.085,.15,.035,5),new Burst(6,3,1.2,.07,.14,.025,6))),
   33         "yitian_sword",new Style("yitian_sword",6,10,0xC6D9E0,Shape.SPARK,List.of(
   34             new Burst(0,2,.65,.11,.25,.018,4),new Burst(2,2,2.2,.11,.25,.018,4),new Burst(4,2,.1,.14,.3,.018,6))));
   35 
   36     public static Set<String> weapons(){return STYLES.keySet();}
   37     public static Style style(String id){return STYLES.get(id);}
   38     public static boolean stamp(net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag data,long now) {
   39         if(data.contains("cod3_material_impact_at")&&data.getLong("cod3_material_impact_at")==now)return false;
   40         data.putLong("cod3_material_impact_at",now);return true;
   41     }
   42     public static Cod3VisualPacket packet(String dimension,String weapon,long seed,long start,Vec3 origin,Vec3 direction) {
   43         var s=style(weapon);return s==null?null:new Cod3VisualPacket(dimension,29,-1,seed,start,s.duration(),1,origin,direction,PREFIX+weapon,s.code(),s.color());
   44     }
   45     public static boolean accepts(Cod3VisualPacket p) {
   46         if(p==null||!p.valid()||!p.sequence().startsWith(PREFIX))return false;
   47         var s=style(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()));
   48         return s!=null&&p.template()==29&&p.entityId()==-1&&p.duration()==s.duration()&&p.scale()==1&&p.tick()==s.code()&&p.tint()==s.color();
   49     }
   50     public static void sendIfSupported(ServerLevel level,String weapon,Vec3 origin,Vec3 direction) {
   51         if(style(weapon)==null)return;
   52         var p=packet(level.dimension().location().toString(),weapon,level.random.nextLong(),level.getGameTime(),origin,direction);
   53         if(!accepts(p))return;
   54         DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(origin.x,origin.y,origin.z,32,level.dimension())),p);
   55     }
   56     /** Observe final primary damage, with an independent bounded cosmetic stamp. */
   57     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   58     public static void hit(LivingDamageEvent event) {
   59         if(event.isCanceled()||event.getAmount()<=0||!(event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)
   60                 ||!(event.getEntity().level() instanceof ServerLevel level))return;
   61         var weapon=EquipmentFeedback.sourceWeapon(event);var id=ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(weapon.getItem());
   62         if(weapon.isEmpty()||id==null||!id.getNamespace().equals(Dynasty.MODID)||style(id.getPath())==null)return;
   63         // sourceWeapon excludes spells, proc damage and held-item substitution;
   64         // these cues additionally reject every projectile/indirect hit.
   65         if(event.getSource().getDirectEntity()!=player||!event.getSource().is(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK))return;
   66         var data=player.getPersistentData();long now=level.getGameTime();
   67         if(!stamp(data,now))return;
   68         sendIfSupported(level,id.getPath(),event.getEntity().position().add(0,event.getEntity().getBbHeight()*.55,0),player.getLookAngle());
   69     }
   70 
   71     /** Exactly the triangles consumed by the client, also inspectable without GLFW. */
   72     public static List<Triangle> geometry(Cod3VisualPacket p,double age) {
   73         if(!accepts(p)||!Double.isFinite(age)||age<0||age>=p.duration())return List.of();
   74         var s=style(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()));var result=new ArrayList<Triangle>();
   75         var right=SevenStarFlash.right(p.direction());var forward=new Vec3(-right.z,0,right.x);var up=new Vec3(0,1,0);
   76         double spin=((p.seed()>>>8)&255)/255.*.32;
   77         for(var burst:s.bursts()) {
   78             double local=age-burst.onset();if(local<0||local>=burst.life())continue;
   79             double alpha=1-local/burst.life();
   80             for(int i=0;i<burst.count();i++) {
   81                 double angle=burst.rotation()+spin+i*Math.PI*2/burst.count();
   82                 Vec3 radial=right.scale(Math.cos(angle)).add(up.scale(Math.sin(angle)));
   83                 Vec3 center=p.origin().add(radial.scale(.06+local*burst.speed())).add(forward.scale(.045*local));
   84                 if(s.shape()==Shape.CHIP)center=center.add(0,-.008*local*local,0);
   85                 double length=burst.length()*(.7+.3*alpha);
   86                 Vec3 axis=s.shape()==Shape.CHIP?radial.add(forward.scale(.5)).normalize():radial;
   87                 Vec3 a=center.subtract(axis.scale(length*.5)),b=center.add(axis.scale(length*.5));
   88                 if(s.shape()==Shape.CHIP)box(result,a,b,burst.width(),s.color(),alpha);
   89                 else spark(result,a,b,burst.width(),s.color(),alpha);
   90             }
   91         }
   92         return List.copyOf(result);
   93     }
   94     private static Vec3 side(Vec3 axis) {
   95         var s=axis.cross(new Vec3(0,1,0));
   96         return s.lengthSqr()<.000001?new Vec3(1,0,0):s.normalize();
   97     }
   98     private static void spark(List<Triangle> out,Vec3 a,Vec3 b,double width,int color,double alpha) {
   99         var axis=b.subtract(a).normalize();var s=side(axis).scale(width);var u=axis.cross(s).normalize().scale(width);
  100         quad(out,a.add(s),b.add(s),b.subtract(s),a.subtract(s),color,alpha);
  101         quad(out,a.add(u),b.add(u),b.subtract(u),a.subtract(u),color,alpha);
  102     }
  103     private static void box(List<Triangle> out,Vec3 a,Vec3 b,double width,int color,double alpha) {
  104         var axis=b.subtract(a).normalize();var s=side(axis).scale(width);var u=axis.cross(s).normalize().scale(width);
  105         Vec3 a0=a.add(s).add(u),a1=a.subtract(s).add(u),a2=a.subtract(s).subtract(u),a3=a.add(s).subtract(u);
  106         Vec3 b0=b.add(s).add(u),b1=b.subtract(s).add(u),b2=b.subtract(s).subtract(u),b3=b.add(s).subtract(u);
  107         quad(out,a0,a1,a2,a3,shade(color,.68),alpha);quad(out,b3,b2,b1,b0,color,alpha);
  108         quad(out,a0,b0,b1,a1,color,alpha);quad(out,a1,b1,b2,a2,shade(color,.82),alpha);
  109         quad(out,a2,b2,b3,a3,shade(color,.68),alpha);quad(out,a3,b3,b0,a0,shade(color,.82),alpha);
  110     }
  111     private static int shade(int color,double factor) {
  112         return ((int)((color>>16&255)*factor)<<16)|((int)((color>>8&255)*factor)<<8)|(int)((color&255)*factor);
  113     }
  114     private static void quad(List<Triangle> out,Vec3 a,Vec3 b,Vec3 c,Vec3 d,int color,double alpha) {
  115         out.add(new Triangle(a,b,c,color,alpha));out.add(new Triangle(a,c,d,color,alpha));
  116     }
  117     private WeaponImpactVisual() {}
  118 }

   20 /** Presentation receipt from real morale reduction; never changes combat values. */
   21 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID)
   22 public final class ArmorCrack {
   23     public static final String PREFIX="equipment_morale_crack_";
   24     public static final int DURATION=10,DARK=0x3C4045;
   25     private record Receipt(DamageSource source,UUID attacker,UUID victim,String dimension,long tick,String weapon) {}
   26     private static final List<Receipt> PENDING=new ArrayList<>();
   27     private static final String[] MASK={"........",".CCCCCC.",".CCC.DC.",".CC.DCC.",".CCD.CC.",".CC.DCC.",".CD.CCC.","........"};
   28     public static int color(String weapon){return "pojun_axe".equals(weapon)?0xD2A164:"xuantian_axe".equals(weapon)?0xA09ABD:-1;}
   29     /** Called only next to the unchanged toughness *= .5 operation. */
   30     public static void reduced(LivingHurtEvent event,LivingEntity attacker,String weapon,double before,double after) {
   31         if(color(weapon)<0||event.isCanceled()||event.getAmount()<=0||!(attacker instanceof ServerPlayer player)
   32                 ||!(event.getEntity().level() instanceof ServerLevel level)||!Double.isFinite(before)||before<=0
   33                 ||before!=com.dynasty.DynastyBalance.mobToughness(event.getEntity().getType())
   34                 ||!Double.isFinite(after)||after!=before*.5||event.getSource().getDirectEntity()!=player||event.getSource().getEntity()!=player
   35                 ||!event.getSource().is(DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK))return;
   36         if(PENDING.size()>=32)PENDING.remove(0);
   37         PENDING.add(new Receipt(event.getSource(),player.getUUID(),event.getEntity().getUUID(),level.dimension().location().toString(),level.getGameTime(),weapon));
   38     }
   39     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   40     public static void finalDamage(LivingDamageEvent event) {
   41         Receipt receipt=null;
   42         for(int i=PENDING.size()-1;i>=0;i--) {
   43             var candidate=PENDING.get(i);
   44             if(candidate.source()==event.getSource()&&candidate.victim().equals(event.getEntity().getUUID())){receipt=PENDING.remove(i);break;}
   45         }
   46         if(receipt==null||event.isCanceled()||event.getAmount()<=0||!(event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)
   47                 ||!(event.getEntity().level() instanceof ServerLevel level)||receipt.tick()!=level.getGameTime()
   48                 ||!receipt.attacker().equals(player.getUUID())||!receipt.dimension().equals(level.dimension().location().toString()))return;
   49         var data=player.getPersistentData();long now=level.getGameTime();
   50         if(data.contains("cod3_armor_crack_at")&&data.getLong("cod3_armor_crack_at")==now)return;
   51         data.putLong("cod3_armor_crack_at",now);
   52         var victim=event.getEntity();var direction=TangWind.horizontal(player.getLookAngle(),Vec3.ZERO);
   53         var origin=anchor(victim.position(),victim.getBbWidth(),victim.getBbHeight(),direction);
   54         var packet=packet(level.dimension().location().toString(),victim.getId(),receipt.weapon(),level.random.nextLong(),now,
   55                 Math.max(.45,Math.min(1.2,victim.getBbWidth()*.8)),origin,direction);
   56         DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(origin.x,origin.y,origin.z,32,level.dimension())),packet);
   57     }
   58     @SubscribeEvent public static void clear(TickEvent.ServerTickEvent event){if(event.phase==TickEvent.Phase.END)PENDING.clear();}
   59     @SubscribeEvent public static void stopped(net.minecraftforge.event.server.ServerStoppedEvent event){PENDING.clear();}
   60     public static Vec3 anchor(Vec3 feet,double width,double height,Vec3 direction) {
   61         return feet.add(0,height*.55,0).subtract(TangWind.horizontal(direction,Vec3.ZERO).scale(width*.5+.025));
   62     }
   63     public static Cod3VisualPacket packet(String dimension,int entityId,String weapon,long seed,long start,double scale,Vec3 origin,Vec3 direction) {
   64         if(color(weapon)<0)return null;
   65         return new Cod3VisualPacket(dimension,27,entityId,seed,start,DURATION,scale,origin,direction,PREFIX+weapon,0,color(weapon));
   66     }
   67     public static boolean accepts(Cod3VisualPacket p) {
   68         if(p==null||!p.valid()||!p.sequence().startsWith(PREFIX))return false;
   69         int tint=color(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()));
   70         return tint>=0&&p.tint()==tint&&p.template()==27&&p.entityId()>=0&&p.tick()==0&&p.duration()==DURATION&&p.scale()>=.45&&p.scale()<=1.2;
   71     }
   72     public static List<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle> geometry(Cod3VisualPacket p,double age,Vec3 center) {
   73         if(!accepts(p)||!Double.isFinite(age)||age<0||age>=DURATION||center==null||!Double.isFinite(center.lengthSqr()))return List.of();
   74         var normal=TangWind.horizontal(p.direction(),Vec3.ZERO);var right=new Vec3(normal.z,0,-normal.x);var up=new Vec3(0,1,0);
   75         var out=new ArrayList<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle>();double cell=p.scale()/8,split=Math.min(.09,age*.014)*p.scale(),alpha=(1-age/DURATION)*.9;
   76         for(int y=1;y<7;y++)for(int x=1;x<7;x++) {
   77             char pixel=MASK[y].charAt(x);if(pixel=='.')continue;
   78             double shift=pixel=='D'?0:(x<4?-split:split);
   79             rect(out,center,right,up,(x-4)*cell+shift,(3-y)*cell,cell,cell,pixel=='D'?DARK:p.tint(),alpha);
   80         }
   81         for(int i=0;i<4;i++) {
   82             double side=i%2==0?-1:1;
   83             rect(out,center,right,up,side*(.25+age*.035)*p.scale(),(.2-i*.12+age*.04-age*age*.004)*p.scale(),cell*.6,cell*.6,p.tint(),alpha);
   84         }
   85         return List.copyOf(out);
   86     }
   87     private static void rect(List<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle> out,Vec3 c,Vec3 r,Vec3 u,double x,double y,double w,double h,int color,double alpha) {
   88         var a=c.add(r.scale(x)).add(u.scale(y));var b=a.add(r.scale(w));var d=a.add(u.scale(h));var e=b.add(u.scale(h));
   89         out.add(new WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle(a,b,e,color,alpha));out.add(new WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle(a,e,d,color,alpha));
   90     }
   91     private ArmorCrack() {}
   92 }

   23 /** Observe a real native hit and real subsequent ascent. Never pushes, moves or damages entities. */
   24 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID)
   25 public final class WeaponLift {
   26     public static final String PREFIX="equipment_confirmed_lift_",STAMP="cod3_confirmed_lift_at";
   27     public static final int DURATION=8;
   28     private static final Set<String> LONGARMS=Set.of("mu_mao","chang_qiang","dragon_spear","bawang_spear","thunder_spear","beichen_spear","baihu_glaive");
   29     private static final List<Candidate> CANDIDATES=new ArrayList<>();
   30     private static final List<Flight> FLIGHTS=new ArrayList<>();
   31     public record Ground(Vec3 point,int color) {}
   32     /** Actual collider support, including slab top height; no dust in unsupported air or fluid. */
   33     public static Ground groundSupport(LivingEntity target) {
   34         if(!(target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)||!target.onGround()||!Double.isFinite(target.position().lengthSqr()))return null;
   35         var feet=target.position();var hit=level.clip(new ClipContext(feet.add(0,.1,0),feet.add(0,-1.2,0),ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER,ClipContext.Fluid.NONE,target));
   36         if(hit.getType()!=HitResult.Type.BLOCK||Math.abs(hit.getLocation().y-feet.y)>=.15)return null;
   37         int tint=level.getBlockState(hit.getBlockPos()).getMapColor(level,hit.getBlockPos()).col;
   38         return new Ground(hit.getLocation().add(0,.015,0),tint==0?0x9A8C78:tint);
   39     }
   40     private static final class Candidate {
   41         final ServerPlayer player;final LivingEntity target;final ServerLevel level;final String weapon;
   42         final long tick;final Vec3 feet,motion,ground,direction;final int groundColor;DamageSource source;
   43         Candidate(ServerPlayer p,LivingEntity t,ServerLevel l,String w) {
   44             player=p;target=t;level=l;weapon=w;tick=l.getGameTime();feet=t.position();motion=t.getDeltaMovement();
   45             direction=TangWind.horizontal(p.getLookAngle(),Vec3.ZERO);
   46             var support=groundSupport(t);ground=support==null?null:support.point();groundColor=support==null?0:support.color();
   47         }
   48     }
   49     private static final class Flight {
   50         final Candidate hit;boolean armed;boolean sampled;
   51         Flight(Candidate c){hit=c;}
   52     }
   53     public static boolean isLongarm(String id){return id!=null&&LONGARMS.contains(id);}
   54     public static int color(String id){return "juque_sword".equals(id)?0xC9A468:"huan_shou_dao".equals(id)?0x85A9AE:isLongarm(id)?EquipmentFeedback.tint(id):-1;}
   55     private static int template(String weapon){return isLongarm(weapon)?3:34;}
   56     private static String weapon(net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack stack) {
   57         var id=ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(stack.getItem());
   58         return !stack.isEmpty()&&id!=null&&id.getNamespace().equals(Dynasty.MODID)?id.getPath():null;
   59     }
   60     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   61     public static void attack(AttackEntityEvent e) {
   62         if(e.isCanceled()||!(e.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p)||!(e.getTarget() instanceof LivingEntity target)
   63                 ||!(target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)||!target.isAlive())return;
   64         var w=weapon(p.getMainHandItem());if(w==null)return;
   65         CANDIDATES.removeIf(c->c.player==p);if(CANDIDATES.size()>=32)CANDIDATES.remove(0);
   66         CANDIDATES.add(new Candidate(p,target,level,w));
   67     }
   68     private static boolean primary(LivingEntity target,DamageSource source,float amount) {
   69         return amount>0&&Float.isFinite(amount)&&source.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer p&&source.getDirectEntity()==p
   70                 &&source.is(DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK)&&!com.dynasty.DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage()
   71                 &&!com.dynasty.EdictSpells.isSpell(source)&&!com.dynasty.QinglongDescent.isDragonDamage(source)
   72                 &&!com.dynasty.DynastyBowRitual.isSolarDamage(source)&&com.dynasty.DynastySchoolCombat.observedPrimary(p,target);
   73     }
   74     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   75     public static void hurt(LivingHurtEvent e) {
   76         if(e.isCanceled()||!primary(e.getEntity(),e.getSource(),e.getAmount()))return;
   77         for(var c:CANDIDATES)if(c.player==e.getSource().getEntity()&&c.target==e.getEntity()&&c.tick==c.level.getGameTime())c.source=e.getSource();
   78     }
   79     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
   80     public static void damage(LivingDamageEvent e) {
   81         Candidate candidate=null;
   82         for(int i=CANDIDATES.size()-1;i>=0;i--){var c=CANDIDATES.get(i);if(c.target==e.getEntity()&&c.source==e.getSource()){candidate=CANDIDATES.remove(i);break;}}
   83         if(candidate==null||e.isCanceled()||!primary(e.getEntity(),e.getSource(),e.getAmount())||candidate.tick!=candidate.level.getGameTime()
   84                 ||!candidate.weapon.equals(weapon(EquipmentFeedback.sourceWeapon(e))))return;
   85         if(color(candidate.weapon)<0&&com.dynasty.DynastySchoolCombat.primaryLift(candidate.player,candidate.target)<.18)return;
   86         var saved=candidate;FLIGHTS.removeIf(f->f.hit.target==saved.target);if(FLIGHTS.size()>=32)FLIGHTS.remove(0);FLIGHTS.add(new Flight(candidate));
   87     }
   88     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(TickEvent.ServerTickEvent e) {
   89         if(e.phase!=TickEvent.Phase.END)return;
   90         CANDIDATES.clear();
   91         for(var it=FLIGHTS.iterator();it.hasNext();) {
   92             var flight=it.next();var c=flight.hit;long elapsed=c.level.getGameTime()-c.tick;var t=c.target;
   93             if(elapsed<0||elapsed>4||!t.isAlive()||t.isRemoved()||!c.player.isAlive()||c.player.isRemoved()||t.level()!=c.level||c.player.level()!=c.level
   94                     ||t.position().distanceToSqr(c.feet)>16){it.remove();continue;}
   95             if(!flight.sampled) {
   96                 flight.sampled=true;var motion=t.getDeltaMovement();
   97                 flight.armed=Double.isFinite(motion.lengthSqr())&&motion.y>0&&motion.y-c.motion.y>=.18
   98                         &&(t.noPhysics||c.level.noCollision(t,t.getBoundingBox().move(0,.18,0)));
   99                 if(!flight.armed){it.remove();continue;}
  100             }
  101             if(t.getY()-c.feet.y<.02)continue;
  102             it.remove();var data=c.player.getPersistentData();long now=c.level.getGameTime();
  103             if(data.contains(STAMP)&&data.getLong(STAMP)==now)continue;
  104             data.putLong(STAMP,now);
  105             com.dynasty.expansion.CombatFeedback.send(t,com.dynasty.expansion.CombatFeedback.LAUNCH);
  106             var p=packet(c.level.dimension().location().toString(),t.getId(),c.weapon,c.level.random.nextLong(),now,
  107                     Math.max(.45,Math.min(1.2,t.getBbWidth())),c.ground==null?c.feet:c.ground,c.direction,c.groundColor,c.ground!=null);
  108             if(p==null)continue; // Other existing native lifts retain their generic cue without borrowing a weapon arc.
  109             var point=t.position();DynastyNetwork.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.NEAR.with(()->new PacketDistributor.TargetPoint(point.x,point.y,point.z,32,c.level.dimension())),p);
  110         }
  111     }
  112     @SubscribeEvent public static void stopped(net.minecraftforge.event.server.ServerStoppedEvent e){CANDIDATES.clear();FLIGHTS.clear();}
  113     public static Cod3VisualPacket packet(String dim,int target,String weapon,long seed,long start,double scale,Vec3 ground,Vec3 direction,int groundColor,boolean supported) {
  114         if(color(weapon)<0)return null;
  115         return new Cod3VisualPacket(dim,template(weapon),target,seed,start,DURATION,scale,ground,TangWind.horizontal(direction,Vec3.ZERO),PREFIX+weapon,supported?0:1,groundColor);
  116     }
  117     public static boolean accepts(Cod3VisualPacket p) {
  118         return p!=null&&p.valid()&&p.sequence().startsWith(PREFIX)&&color(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()))>=0&&p.template()==template(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()))
  119                 &&p.entityId()>=0&&p.duration()==DURATION&&p.tick()<=1&&p.scale()>=.45&&p.scale()<=1.2
  120                 &&Math.abs(p.direction().y)<1e-6&&p.direction().lengthSqr()>.9&&p.direction().lengthSqr()<1.1;
  121     }
  122     /** Arc has no invented flight path; feet come from the actual interpolated target. */
  123     public static List<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle> geometry(Cod3VisualPacket p,double age,Vec3 feet,boolean airborne) {
  124         if(!accepts(p)||!Double.isFinite(age)||age<0||age>=DURATION||feet==null||!Double.isFinite(feet.lengthSqr()))return List.of();
  125         var out=new ArrayList<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle>();double scale=p.scale(),alpha=(1-age/DURATION)*.85;
  126         var right=new Vec3(p.direction().z,0,-p.direction().x);var up=new Vec3(0,1,0);
  127         if(airborne) {
  128             double[][] path={{-.5,.08},{-.5,.32},{-.38,.53},{-.17,.72},{.08,.85},{.34,.89}};
  129             int tint=color(p.sequence().substring(PREFIX.length()));
  130             for(int i=0;i<path.length-1;i++) {
  131                 var a=feet.add(right.scale(path[i][0]*scale)).add(up.scale(path[i][1]*scale));
  132                 var b=feet.add(right.scale(path[i+1][0]*scale)).add(up.scale(path[i+1][1]*scale));
  133                 var axis=b.subtract(a).normalize();var side=p.direction().cross(axis).normalize().scale(.035*scale);
  134                 quad(out,a.add(side),a.subtract(side),b.subtract(side),b.add(side),tint,alpha);
  135                 var cross=p.direction().scale(.035*scale);quad(out,a.add(cross),a.subtract(cross),b.subtract(cross),b.add(cross),tint,alpha);
  136             }
  137         }
  138         if(p.tick()==0&&age<5)for(int i=0;i<4;i++) {
  139             double angle=i*Math.PI*.5+((p.seed()>>>8)&15)*.04;
  140             var center=p.origin().add(Math.cos(angle)*(.12+age*.045)*scale,.055*scale,Math.sin(angle)*(.12+age*.045)*scale);
  141             cube(out,center,.045*scale,p.tint(),(1-age/5)*.8);
  142         }
  143         return List.copyOf(out);
  144     }
  145     private static void cube(List<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle> out,Vec3 c,double half,int color,double alpha) {
  146         var x=new Vec3(half,0,0);var y=new Vec3(0,half,0);var z=new Vec3(0,0,half);
  147         var v=new Vec3[]{c.subtract(x).subtract(y).subtract(z),c.add(x).subtract(y).subtract(z),c.add(x).add(y).subtract(z),c.subtract(x).add(y).subtract(z),
  148                 c.subtract(x).subtract(y).add(z),c.add(x).subtract(y).add(z),c.add(x).add(y).add(z),c.subtract(x).add(y).add(z)};
  149         int[][] faces={{0,1,2,3},{5,4,7,6},{4,0,3,7},{1,5,6,2},{3,2,6,7},{4,5,1,0}};
  150         for(int i=0;i<6;i++){int[] f=faces[i];quad(out,v[f[0]],v[f[1]],v[f[2]],v[f[3]],i==4?color:shade(color,i==5?.65:.82),alpha);}
  151     }
  152     private static int shade(int c,double f){return ((int)((c>>16&255)*f)<<16)|((int)((c>>8&255)*f)<<8)|(int)((c&255)*f);}
  153     private static void quad(List<WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle> out,Vec3 a,Vec3 b,Vec3 c,Vec3 d,int color,double alpha) {
  154         out.add(new WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle(a,b,c,color,alpha));out.add(new WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle(a,c,d,color,alpha));
  155     }
  156     private WeaponLift() {}
  157 }

   21 /** Material hit geometry on the existing packet; bounded independently of scenic effects. */
   22 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID,value=Dist.CLIENT)
   23 public final class WeaponImpactRenderer {
   24     private static final int LIMIT=12;
   25     private static final List<Cod3VisualPacket> ACTIVE=new ArrayList<>();
   26     private static final BufferBuilder BUFFER=new BufferBuilder(65536);
   27     private static ClientLevel world;
   28 
   29     public static boolean receive(Cod3VisualPacket p) {
   30         if(!p.sequence().startsWith(WeaponImpactVisual.PREFIX))return false;
   31         clean();
   32         if(world==null||!WeaponImpactVisual.accepts(p)||!p.dimension().equals(world.dimension().location().toString()))return true;
   33         long age=world.getGameTime()-p.start();
   34         if(age< -2||age>=p.duration()||ACTIVE.contains(p))return true;
   35         if(ACTIVE.size()>=LIMIT)ACTIVE.remove(0);ACTIVE.add(p);return true;
   36     }
   37     private static void clean() {
   38         var current=Minecraft.getInstance().level;
   39         if(current!=world){ACTIVE.clear();world=current;}
   40         if(world!=null)ACTIVE.removeIf(p->world.getGameTime()-p.start()>=p.duration());
   41     }
   42     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) {
   43         if(e.phase==TickEvent.Phase.END)clean();
   44     }
   45     @SubscribeEvent public static void render(RenderLevelStageEvent e) {
   46         if(e.getStage()!=RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_PARTICLES)return;
   47         clean();if(world==null||ACTIVE.isEmpty())return;
   48         var camera=e.getCamera().getPosition();var pose=e.getPoseStack();pose.pushPose();boolean drawing=false;
   49         try(ImperialRenderState state=new ImperialRenderState()) {
   50             RenderSystem.enableBlend();RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();RenderSystem.enableDepthTest();
   51             RenderSystem.depthMask(false);RenderSystem.disableCull();RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1,1,1,1);
   52             RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);pose.translate(-camera.x,-camera.y,-camera.z);
   53             BUFFER.begin(VertexFormat.Mode.TRIANGLES,DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);drawing=true;
   54             var matrix=pose.last().pose();
   55             for(var p:ACTIVE) {
   56                 if(p.origin().distanceToSqr(camera)>64*64)continue;
   57                 double age=world.getGameTime()-p.start()+e.getPartialTick();
   58                 for(var t:WeaponImpactVisual.geometry(p,age))triangle(matrix,t);
   59             }
   60             BufferUploader.drawWithShader(BUFFER.end());drawing=false;
   61         }finally{if(drawing)BUFFER.discard();pose.popPose();}
   62     }
   63     private static void triangle(Matrix4f m,WeaponImpactVisual.Triangle t) {
   64         for(Vec3 v:new Vec3[]{t.a(),t.b(),t.c()})BUFFER.vertex(m,(float)v.x,(float)v.y,(float)v.z)
   65             .color((t.color()>>16&255)/255f,(t.color()>>8&255)/255f,(t.color()&255)/255f,(float)t.alpha()).endVertex();
   66     }
   67     private WeaponImpactRenderer() {}
   68 }

   26     private static ClientLevel world;
   27     public static boolean receive(Cod3VisualPacket p) {

   31         ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.entityId()==p.entityId());if(ACTIVE.size()>=12)ACTIVE.remove(0);ACTIVE.add(p);return true;

   33     private static void clean() {
   34         var current=Minecraft.getInstance().level;if(current!=world){ACTIVE.clear();world=current;}
   35         if(world!=null)ACTIVE.removeIf(p->world.getGameTime()-p.start()>=p.duration());
   36     }

   53                 }
   54                 if(anchor.distanceToSqr(camera)>64*64)continue;

   56             }

   21 /** Bounded world-space library. Six composable renderers, with no full-screen geometry. */
   22 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid=Dynasty.MODID,value=Dist.CLIENT)
   23 public final class Cod3VfxRenderer {
   24     private static final List<Cod3VisualPacket> ACTIVE=new ArrayList<>();
   25     private static ClientLevel world;
   26     private static long soundTick=Long.MIN_VALUE;
   27     private static int sounds;
   28     private static final BufferBuilder BUFFER=new BufferBuilder(262144);
   29     private static void clean(){
   30         var mc=Minecraft.getInstance();var current=mc.level;
   31         if(current!=world){ACTIVE.clear();Cod3AfterImages.clear();ScenicModels.clear();ScenicArraySound.clear();ScenicDragonSound.clear();ScenicGhostShipSound.clear();ScenicStoneGodSound.clear();ScenicMirageSound.clear();ScenicMirageRenderer.clear();ScenicGreatWallRenderer.clear();ScenicGreatWallSound.clear();ScenicGhostArmyRenderer.clear();ScenicGhostMarketRenderer.clear();ScenicGhostMarketLights.clear();ScenicGhostMarketSound.clear();ScenicDragonPalaceRenderer.clear();ScenicDragonPalaceSound.clear();ScenicFireTreeSound.clear();soundTick=Long.MIN_VALUE;world=current;}
   32         if(world==null)return;
   33         ACTIVE.removeIf(p->{
   34             if(world.getGameTime()-p.start()>=p.duration()||p.start()-world.getGameTime()>40)return true;
   35             if(p.template()==4&&!p.sequence().equals("arrow_collapse")&&p.entityId()>=0){var arrow=world.getEntity(p.entityId());if(arrow==null||!arrow.isAlive())return true;}
   36             if(p.sequence().startsWith("equipment_")||p.sequence().equals("vfx_follow")||Cod3AfterImages.supports(p)||p.sequence().matches("accessory_proc_[13456]")){
   37                 var owner=world.getEntity(p.entityId());
   38                 if(owner==null||!owner.isAlive()||owner.getUUID().getLeastSignificantBits()!=p.seed())return true;
   39                 return p.sequence().equals("equipment_weapon_meteor_charge")&&(!(owner instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity living)||!living.isUsingItem()||!living.getUseItem().is(com.dynasty.expansion.ExpansionContent.HAMMER.get()));
   40             }
   41             if(p.sequence().startsWith("scenic_"))return mc.player==null||mc.player.distanceToSqr(p.origin())>256*256;
   42             if(!p.sequence().startsWith("intro_")&&!p.sequence().startsWith("death_"))return false;
   43             var entity=world.getEntity(p.entityId());
   44             return mc.player==null||entity==null||mc.player.distanceToSqr(entity)>32*32;
   45         });
   46         Cod3AfterImages.retain(ACTIVE);
   47     }
   48     static List<Cod3VisualPacket> scenicPackets(){clean();return List.copyOf(ACTIVE);}
   49     public static void receive(Cod3VisualPacket packet){
   50         clean();if(!packet.valid()||world==null||!packet.dimension().equals(world.dimension().location().toString()))return;
   51         if(packet.sequence().startsWith("status_clear_")){
   52             String status="status_"+packet.sequence().substring("status_clear_".length());
   53             ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.entityId()==packet.entityId()&&old.sequence().equals(status));return;
   54         }
   55         if(packet.sequence().matches("status_[1-8]")||packet.sequence().startsWith("secondary_"))ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.entityId()==packet.entityId()&&old.sequence().equals(packet.sequence()));
   56         if(packet.sequence().equals("equipment_weapon_meteor_charge"))ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.entityId()==packet.entityId()&&old.sequence().equals(packet.sequence()));
   57         if(packet.sequence().equals("arrow_collapse"))ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.template()==4&&old.entityId()==packet.entityId()&&old.seed()==packet.seed());
   58         if(packet.template()==0){
   59             // Typed selection excludes sound, WAIT and SET_* steps from the visual queue.
   60             Cod3Vfx.resume(packet, Cod3Catalog.sequenceById(packet.sequence())).forEach(Cod3VfxRenderer::add);
   61         }else add(packet);
   62     }
   63     private static void add(Cod3VisualPacket p){
   64         if(!p.valid()||p.template()==0)return;
   65         if(p.sequence().equals("scenic_13"))ACTIVE.removeIf(old->old.sequence().equals("scenic_13")&&old.seed()==p.seed()&&old.start()!=p.start());
   66         if(p.sequence().startsWith("combat_")){try{VfxInstance.combat(p);}catch(RuntimeException invalid){return;}}
   67         if(ACTIVE.stream().anyMatch(old->old.entityId()==p.entityId()&&old.start()==p.start()&&old.template()==p.template()&&old.seed()==p.seed()&&old.sequence().equals(p.sequence())))return;
   68         boolean templateCue=p.sequence().isBlank()||p.sequence().equals("vfx_follow")||p.sequence().startsWith("combat_")||Cod3AfterImages.supports(p);
   69         if(templateCue&&ACTIVE.stream().filter(x->x.template()==p.template()&&(p.template()!=29||x.entityId()==p.entityId())).count()>=VfxTimeline.limit(p.template()))return;
   70         if(p.scale()>2&&ACTIVE.stream().filter(x->x.scale()>2).count()>=3)return;
   71         if(ACTIVE.size()>=48)ACTIVE.remove(0);ACTIVE.add(p);Cod3AfterImages.sample(p);
   72         if(templateCue&&p.template()!=4&&world.getGameTime()-p.start()>=0&&world.getGameTime()-p.start()<=2){
   73             long now=world.getGameTime();if(soundTick!=now){soundTick=now;sounds=0;}
   74             if(sounds++<3)world.playLocalSound(p.origin().x,p.origin().y,p.origin().z,VfxTimeline.sound(p.template()),net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.PLAYERS,.4f,1,false);
   75         }
   76     }
   77     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e){
   78         if(e.phase!=TickEvent.Phase.END)return;clean();if(world==null||Minecraft.getInstance().isPaused())return;

   95         // The leaf sound is local, infrequent and tied to the same visible regional clock.
