   29 /** Client-only rig replacement; existing mobs, saves, AI, hitboxes and skills stay authoritative. */
   30 public final class BossCuboidRenderer<E extends Mob> extends GeoReplacedEntityRenderer<E,BossCuboidRenderer.Visual> {
   31     private final boolean celestialAura;
   32     private final boolean royalAura;
   33     public BossCuboidRenderer(EntityRendererProvider.Context context,EntityType<E> type,String id){
   34         super(context,new Model(id),new Visual(type,id));
   35         celestialAura=id.equals("nine_heaven_general");
   36         royalAura=id.equals("dragon_king")||id.equals("dragon_emperor");
   37         // The three human bosses author their real size in Cuboid units, without a species scale.
   38         shadowRadius=id.equals("dragon_emperor")?.85f:id.equals("dragon_king")?.7f:id.equals("nine_heaven_general")?.8f:.5f;
   39         if(id.equals("nine_heaven_general")||id.equals("dragon_king")||id.equals("dragon_emperor"))addRenderLayer(new GeoRenderLayer<Visual>(this){
   40             @Override public void render(PoseStack pose,Visual visual,BakedGeoModel model,RenderType type,MultiBufferSource buffers,VertexConsumer vertex,float partial,int light,int overlay){
   41                 var mob=getCurrentEntity();
   42                 var controller=visual.getAnimatableInstanceCache().getManagerForId(mob.getId()).getAnimationControllers().get("body");
   43                 boolean casting=controller!=null&&controller.getCurrentAnimation()!=null&&controller.getCurrentAnimation().animation().name().equals("cast");
   44                 BossHumanAura.draw(pose,buffers,mob,id,partial,casting);
   45                 if(id.equals("nine_heaven_general")){
   46                     float alpha=TianjiangAura.glowAlpha(mob,partial,casting);
   47                     if(alpha>0){
   48                         var glow=RenderType.entityTranslucentEmissive(TianjiangAura.GLOW);
   49                         BossCuboidRenderer.this.reRender(model,pose,buffers,visual,glow,buffers.getBuffer(glow),partial,
   50                                 15728880,net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.OverlayTexture.NO_OVERLAY,1,1,1,alpha);
   51                     }
   52                 }
   53             }
   54         });
   55         if(celestialAura||royalAura)addRenderLayer(new GeoRenderLayer<Visual>(this){
   56             @Override public void renderForBone(PoseStack pose,Visual visual,GeoBone bone,RenderType type,MultiBufferSource buffers,VertexConsumer vertex,float partial,int light,int overlay){
   57                 if(!bone.getName().equals("weapon")||bone.isHidden())return;
   58                 pose.pushPose();
   59                 // The recursive renderer already applied the animated weapon rotation.
   60                 software.bernie.geckolib.util.RenderUtils.translateToPivotPoint(pose,bone);
   61                 if(celestialAura)TianjiangAura.drawWeapon(pose,buffers,getCurrentEntity(),partial);
   62                 else RoyalBossAura.drawWeapon(pose,buffers,getCurrentEntity(),partial,id.equals("dragon_king"));
   63                 buffers.getBuffer(type);
   64                 pose.popPose();
   65             }
   66         });
   67         // Preserve actual main/offhand equipment at articulated grip bones.
   68         addRenderLayer(new BlockAndItemGeoLayer<Visual>(this){
   69             @Override protected ItemStack getStackForBone(GeoBone bone,Visual visual){
   70                 boolean left=bone.getName().equals("left_grip");
   71                 if(!left&&!bone.getName().equals("right_grip"))return null;
   72                 E mob=getCurrentEntity();
   73                 return left==(mob.getMainArm()==HumanoidArm.LEFT)?mob.getMainHandItem():mob.getOffhandItem();
   74             }
   75             @Override protected void renderStackForBone(PoseStack pose,GeoBone bone,ItemStack item,Visual visual,MultiBufferSource buffers,float partial,int light,int overlay){
   76                 if(item.isEmpty())return;
   77                 boolean left=bone.getName().equals("left_grip");
   78                 // This rig is already Y-up in Gecko's bone space. Vanilla's
   79                 // humanoid inversion would turn upright blades into flat ones.
   80                 if(item.is(Items.TRIDENT)){
   81                     // The built-in shaft runs from Y=2 to Y=27 before its
   82                     // renderer flips Y. Center that shaft on the actual palm;
   83                     // the vanilla humanoid display transform assumes another basis.
   84                     pose.translate(0,14.0/16.0,0);
   85                     net.minecraftforge.client.extensions.common.IClientItemExtensions.of(item).getCustomRenderer()
   86                             .renderByItem(item,ItemDisplayContext.NONE,pose,buffers,light,overlay);
   87                     return;
   88                 }
   89                 context.getItemInHandRenderer().renderItem(getCurrentEntity(),item,left?ItemDisplayContext.THIRD_PERSON_LEFT_HAND:ItemDisplayContext.THIRD_PERSON_RIGHT_HAND,left,pose,buffers,light);
   90             }
   91         });
   92     }
   93     @Override public boolean shouldRender(E mob,net.minecraft.client.renderer.culling.Frustum frustum,double x,double y,double z){
   94         if(super.shouldRender(mob,frustum,x,y,z))return true;
   95         // Expand only visual culling; the accepted collision and damage bounds do not change.
   96         return (celestialAura||royalAura)&&!mob.isInvisible()&&!mob.isDeadOrDying()&&mob.shouldRender(x,y,z)
   97                 &&frustum.isVisible(mob.getBoundingBox().inflate(3,2,3));
   98     }
   99     @Override protected float getDeathMaxRotation(Visual visual){
  100         // Every species now authors its grounded fall; avoid a second feet-pivot flip.
  101         return 0;
  102     }

  104     public static final class Visual implements GeoReplacedEntity {
  105         private final EntityType<?> type;
  106         private final boolean humanBoss;
  107         private final AnimatableInstanceCache cache=GeckoLibUtil.createInstanceCache(this);
  108         private static final RawAnimation IDLE=RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop("idle"),WALK=RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop("walk"),
  109                 ATTACK=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay("attack"),HURT=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay("hurt"),DEATH=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlayAndHold("death");
  110         Visual(EntityType<?> type,String id){this.type=type;this.humanBoss=id.equals("nine_heaven_general")||id.equals("dragon_king")||id.equals("dragon_emperor");}
  111         @Override public EntityType<?> getReplacingEntityType(){return type;}
  112         @Override public AnimatableInstanceCache getAnimatableInstanceCache(){return cache;}
  113         @Override public void registerControllers(AnimatableManager.ControllerRegistrar controllers){
  114             var controller=new AnimationController<Visual>(this,"body",2,state->{
  115                 if(!(state.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY) instanceof Mob mob))return PlayState.STOP;
  116                 // A six-tick strike must not spend its first two ticks blending away
  117                 // its tell/contact. This only changes visual transitions for the three
  118                 // approved human bosses; server swing and damage timing stay intact.
  119                 if(humanBoss)state.getController().setTransitionLength(mob.swinging||mob.isDeadOrDying()||mob.hurtTime>0?0:2);
  120                 if(mob.isDeadOrDying())return state.setAndContinue(DEATH);
  121                 if(mob.hurtTime>0)return state.setAndContinue(HURT);
  122                 if(mob.swinging)return state.setAndContinue(ATTACK);
  123                 var playing=state.getController().getCurrentAnimation();
  124                 if(playing!=null&&(playing.animation().name().equals("attack")||playing.animation().name().equals("hurt"))&&!state.getController().hasAnimationFinished())return PlayState.CONTINUE;
  125                 return state.setAndContinue(state.isMoving()?WALK:IDLE);
  126             });
  127             // Local resource-QA triggers; never invoked by production gameplay.
  128             for(String clip:new String[]{"idle","walk","attack","cast","hurt","death"})
  129                 controller.triggerableAnim("art_check_"+clip,RawAnimation.begin().then(clip,Animation.LoopType.HOLD_ON_LAST_FRAME));
  130             controllers.add(controller);
  131         }
  132     }
  133 
  134     private static final class Model extends GeoModel<Visual>{
  135         private final ResourceLocation geo,texture,animation;
  136         private final boolean humanBoss;
  137         Model(String id){humanBoss=id.equals("nine_heaven_general")||id.equals("dragon_king")||id.equals("dragon_emperor");geo=new ResourceLocation("dynasty","geo/boss_cuboid/"+id+".geo.json");texture=new ResourceLocation("dynasty","textures/entity/boss_cuboid/"+id+".png");animation=new ResourceLocation("dynasty","animations/boss_cuboid/"+id+".animation.json");}
  138         @Override public ResourceLocation getModelResource(Visual visual){return geo;}
  139         @Override public ResourceLocation getTextureResource(Visual visual){return texture;}
  140         @Override public ResourceLocation getAnimationResource(Visual visual){return animation;}
  141         @Override public void setCustomAnimations(Visual visual,long instanceId,AnimationState<Visual> state){
  142             // The authored spear/trident is a visual carrier for existing skills. Actual
  143             // equipped main-hand items use the grip layer instead, never two overlapping weapons.
  144             if(humanBoss&&state.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY) instanceof Mob equipped)
  145                 getBone("weapon").ifPresent(weapon->weapon.setHidden(!equipped.getMainHandItem().isEmpty()));
  146             if(state.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY) instanceof Mob mob&&mob.isDeadOrDying())return;
  147             var data=state.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY_MODEL_DATA);
  148             if(data==null)return;
  149             getBone("head").ifPresent(head->{
  150                 head.setRotY(head.getRotY()+Mth.clamp(data.netHeadYaw()*Mth.DEG_TO_RAD,-.6f,.6f));
  151                 head.setRotX(head.getRotX()+Mth.clamp(data.headPitch()*Mth.DEG_TO_RAD,-.35f,.35f));
  152             });
  153         }
  154     }
  155 }

   61     public static final class Chain extends GeoEntityRenderer<com.dynasty.blueprint.elite.HammerChain> {
   62         public Chain(EntityRendererProvider.Context context) { super(context,new Model<>("hammer_chain")); shadowRadius=.65f; }
   63         @Override public void render(com.dynasty.blueprint.elite.HammerChain actor,float yaw,float partial,PoseStack pose,MultiBufferSource buffers,int light) {
   64             super.render(actor,yaw,partial,pose,buffers,light);
   65             if(!(actor.level().getEntity(actor.ownerEntityId()) instanceof com.dynasty.blueprint.elite.HammerGuardian caster)||!caster.isAlive())return;
   66             double age=caster.level().getGameTime()-caster.skillStart()+partial;
   67             double angle=age<36?-30:age<48?-30*(48-age)/12:0;
   68             Vec3 endpoint=com.dynasty.entity.BossFormSockets.hand(caster,caster.getPosition(partial),angle);
   69             Vec3 to=endpoint.subtract(actor.getPosition(partial));
   70             if(to.lengthSqr()>32*32)return;
   71             pose.pushPose();
   72             try {
   73                 var entry=pose.last();var vertices=buffers.getBuffer(net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderType.lines());
   74                 for(int i=0;i<8;i++) {
   75                     double a=i/8.0,b=(i+1)/8.0;
   76                     Vec3 from=to.scale(a).add(0,-.15*Math.sin(Math.PI*a),0),end=to.scale(b).add(0,-.15*Math.sin(Math.PI*b),0),normal=end.subtract(from).normalize();
   77                     vertices.vertex(entry.pose(),(float)from.x,(float)from.y,(float)from.z).color(112,108,93,255).normal(entry.normal(),(float)normal.x,(float)normal.y,(float)normal.z).endVertex();
   78                     vertices.vertex(entry.pose(),(float)end.x,(float)end.y,(float)end.z).color(112,108,93,255).normal(entry.normal(),(float)normal.x,(float)normal.y,(float)normal.z).endVertex();
   79                 }
   80             } finally { pose.popPose(); }
   81         }
   82     }

   84     public static final class CoreVisual implements GeoReplacedEntity {
   85         private final EntityType<?> type;
   86         private final AnimatableInstanceCache cache=GeckoLibUtil.createInstanceCache(this);
   87         private static final RawAnimation IDLE=RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop("idle"),WALK=RawAnimation.begin().thenLoop("walk"),
   88             ATTACK=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay("attack"),HURT=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlay("hurt"),DEATH=RawAnimation.begin().thenPlayAndHold("death");
   89         CoreVisual(EntityType<?> type) { this.type=type; }
   90         @Override public EntityType<?> getReplacingEntityType() { return type; }
   91         @Override public AnimatableInstanceCache getAnimatableInstanceCache() { return cache; }
   92         @Override public void registerControllers(AnimatableManager.ControllerRegistrar controllers) {
   93             final Mob[] active=new Mob[1];final float[] partial=new float[1];
   94             var controller=new AnimationController<CoreVisual>(this,"body",0,state -> {
   95                 if(!(state.getData(DataTickets.ENTITY) instanceof Mob mob))return PlayState.STOP;
   96                 active[0]=mob;partial[0]=state.getPartialTick();
   97                 if(mob.isDeadOrDying())return state.setAndContinue(DEATH);
   98                 if(mob.hurtTime>0)return state.setAndContinue(HURT);
   99                 boolean winding=mob instanceof com.dynasty.entity.DynastyBosses.RebelGeneral rebel && rebel.characterAttackProgress(partial[0])>0;
  100                 if(mob.swinging||winding)return state.setAndContinue(ATTACK);
  101                 var playing=state.getController().getCurrentAnimation();
  102                 if(playing!=null && (playing.animation().name().equals("attack")||playing.animation().name().equals("hurt")) && !state.getController().hasAnimationFinished())return PlayState.CONTINUE;
  103                 return state.setAndContinue(state.isMoving()?WALK:IDLE);
  104             }) {
  105                 @Override protected double adjustTick(double tick) {
  106                     double local=super.adjustTick(tick);
  107                     if(active[0] instanceof com.dynasty.entity.DynastyBosses.RebelGeneral rebel && getCurrentAnimation()!=null && getCurrentAnimation().animation().name().equals("attack")) {
  108                         float progress=rebel.characterAttackProgress(partial[0]);
  109                         if(progress>0)return progress*rebel.characterAttackDuration();
  110                     }
  111                     return local;
  112                 }
  113             };
  114             for(String clip:new String[]{"idle","walk","attack","cast","hurt","death"})
  115                 controller.triggerableAnim("art_check_"+clip,RawAnimation.begin().then(clip,Animation.LoopType.HOLD_ON_LAST_FRAME));
  116             controllers.add(controller);
  117         }
  118     }

  120     public static final class Core<E extends Mob> extends GeoReplacedEntityRenderer<E,CoreVisual> {
  121         public Core(EntityRendererProvider.Context context,EntityType<E> type,String id) {
  122             super(context,new Model<>(id),new CoreVisual(type));
  123             shadowRadius=id.equals("eunuch_mastermind")?1.6f:1.9f;
  124             if(id.equals("rebel_general"))addRenderLayer(new BlockAndItemGeoLayer<CoreVisual>(this) {
  125                 @Override protected ItemStack getStackForBone(GeoBone bone,CoreVisual actor) {
  126                     return bone.getName().equals("right_grip")?getCurrentEntity().getMainHandItem():null;
  127                 }
  128                 @Override protected void renderStackForBone(PoseStack pose,GeoBone bone,ItemStack item,CoreVisual actor,
  129                         MultiBufferSource buffers,float partial,int light,int overlay) {
  130                     if(item.isEmpty())return;
  131                     pose.pushPose();
  132                     try {
  133                         pose.scale(1.8f,1.8f,1.8f);
  134                         context.getItemInHandRenderer().renderItem(getCurrentEntity(),item,ItemDisplayContext.THIRD_PERSON_RIGHT_HAND,false,pose,buffers,light);
  135                     } finally { pose.popPose(); }
  136                 }
  137             });
  138         }
  139         @Override protected float getDeathMaxRotation(CoreVisual actor) { return 0; }
  140         @Override public boolean shouldRender(E actor,Frustum frustum,double x,double y,double z) {
  141             return actor.shouldRender(x,y,z) && (actor.noCulling || frustum.isVisible(actor.getBoundingBox().inflate(5,3,5)));
  142         }
  143     }

   38 /** Four opt-in, bounded combat loops; server-only state, no recursive damage or world edits. */
   39 @Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = Dynasty.MODID)
   40 public final class DynastySchoolCombat {
   41     public static final String STAR_ARROW = "dynasty_star_arrow", FIRING_WEAPON = "dynasty_star_weapon";
   42     private static final String PROCESSED = "dynasty_star_processed";
   43     private static final UUID SILK_RESISTANCE = UUID.fromString("e48ea587-a708-43e5-ab60-c427ed51bcf9");
   44     private static final Map<UUID, State> STATES = new HashMap<>();
   45     static final Set<String> ACCESSORIES = Set.of("zhenguan_mirror", "huben_bracer", "liancheng_tassel", "tayun_pendant",
   46             "guanxing_pendant", "mingxian_ring", "sitian_seal", "dingfeng_silk");
   47 
   48     private DynastySchoolCombat() { }
   49     static final class State {
   50         UUID attacked, primaryTarget, comboTarget, marked;
   51         long attackTick = -1, counterUntil = -1, comboTick = -1, markedTick = -1, chargeStart = -1, edictUntil = -1;
   52         float attackStrength;
   53         int combo;
   54         String attackWeapon = "", held = "";
   55         Vec3 origin,primaryMotion=Vec3.ZERO;
   56         ItemStack primaryStack=ItemStack.EMPTY;
   57         boolean edictHadTarget,primaryCritical;
   58     }
   59     static State state(Player player) { return STATES.computeIfAbsent(player.getUUID(), key -> new State()); }
   60     static long now(Player player) { return player.level().getGameTime(); }
   61     static boolean has(Player player, String id) { return DynastyTrinkets.activeIds(player).contains(id); }
   62     static String weapon(Player player) { return String.valueOf(DynastyTrinkets.idOf(player.getMainHandItem())); }
   63     static boolean hostile(Player player, LivingEntity target) {
   64         return target.isAlive() && target instanceof Enemy && !(target instanceof Player)
   65                 && target != player && !target.isAlliedTo(player) && !player.isAlliedTo(target);
   66     }
   67     static boolean boss(LivingEntity target) {
   68         // Boss identity is not a toughness threshold: the First Emperor has 0.84 toughness.
   69         return target instanceof DynastyBossCombat.BarHolder
   70                 || !target.canChangeDimensions() || DynastyBalance.isBossOrBeast(target.getType());
   71     }
   72     public static boolean isStarArrow(DamageSource source) {
   73         return source.getDirectEntity() instanceof AbstractArrow arrow && arrow.getPersistentData().getBoolean(STAR_ARROW);
   74     }
   75     public static ItemStack firingWeapon(DamageSource source, ItemStack held) {
   76         if(source.getDirectEntity() instanceof AbstractArrow a && a.getPersistentData().contains("cod4FiringWeapon"))
   77             return ItemStack.of(a.getPersistentData().getCompound("cod4FiringWeapon"));
   78         if (!isStarArrow(source)) {
   79             if(source.getDirectEntity() instanceof AbstractArrow && source.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
   80                 var snapshot=DynastyWeaponProgression.attackWeapon(player,source);
   81                 if(!snapshot.isEmpty())return snapshot;
   82             }
   83             return held;
   84         }
   85         return ItemStack.of(source.getDirectEntity().getPersistentData().getCompound(FIRING_WEAPON));
   86     }
   87 
   88     @SubscribeEvent
   89     public static void attack(AttackEntityEvent event) {
   90         Player player = event.getEntity();
   91         if (player.level().isClientSide) return;
   92         State s = state(player);
   93         String held = weapon(player);
   94         if (!held.equals(s.held)) resetOnSwap(player, s, held);
   95         // Vanilla resets attackStrengthTicker before LivingHurtEvent: capture before Player.attack instead.
   96         s.attacked = event.getTarget().getUUID();
   97         s.primaryTarget = s.attacked;
   98         s.attackTick = now(player);
   99         s.attackStrength = player.getAttackStrengthScale(0.5F);
  100         s.attackWeapon = held;
  101         s.primaryStack=player.getMainHandItem().copy();s.primaryMotion=event.getTarget().getDeltaMovement();s.primaryCritical=false;
  102         if (!held.equals("liuyun_sword") || !SchoolCombatRules.fullAttack(s.attackStrength)
  103                 || !event.getTarget().getUUID().equals(s.comboTarget)) s.combo = 0;
  104     }
  105 
  106     /** Vanilla resets the cooldown before Hurt; reuse the pre-attack capture for armor/accessory combo hooks. */
  107     public static boolean chargedPrimary(Player player,LivingEntity target) {
  108         State s=STATES.get(player.getUUID());
  109         if(s==null || s.attackTick!=now(player))return player.getAttackStrengthScale(.5F)>.8F;
  110         return target.getUUID().equals(s.primaryTarget) && weapon(player).equals(s.attackWeapon) && s.attackStrength>.8F;
  111     }
  112 
  113     /** Existing pre-attack context also supplies immutable, source-correct presentation. */
  114     public static boolean observedPrimary(Player p,LivingEntity target){
  115         var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());return s!=null&&s.attackTick==now(p)&&target.getUUID().equals(s.primaryTarget);
  116     }
  117     public static ItemStack primaryStack(Player p,LivingEntity target){var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());return observedPrimary(p,target)?s.primaryStack.copy():ItemStack.EMPTY;}
  118     public static boolean primaryCritical(Player p,LivingEntity target){var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());return observedPrimary(p,target)&&s.primaryCritical;}
  119     public static double primaryLift(Player p,LivingEntity target){
  120         var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());if(!observedPrimary(p,target))return 0;
  121         double added=target.getDeltaMovement().y-s.primaryMotion.y;
  122         // A roof/occupied body space must not advertise a launch that collision prevents.
  123         return added>=.18&&(target.noPhysics||target.level().noCollision(target,target.getBoundingBox().move(0,.18,0)))?added:0;
  124     }
  125     public static boolean primaryCharged(Player p,LivingEntity target){var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());return observedPrimary(p,target)&&s.attackStrength>.8F;}
  126     @SubscribeEvent(priority=EventPriority.LOWEST)
  127     public static void critical(net.minecraftforge.event.entity.player.CriticalHitEvent e){
  128         var p=e.getEntity();var s=STATES.get(p.getUUID());
  129         if(p.level().isClientSide||s==null||s.attackTick!=now(p)||!e.getTarget().getUUID().equals(s.primaryTarget))return;
  130         var result=e.getResult();s.primaryCritical=e.getDamageModifier()>1&&result!=net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.Event.Result.DENY
  131                 &&(result==net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.Event.Result.ALLOW||e.isVanillaCritical());
  132     }
  133 
  134     @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.LOW)
  135     public static void block(ShieldBlockEvent event) {
  136         if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) || player.level().isClientSide
  137                 || !(player.getUseItem().getItem() instanceof DynastySchoolWeapons.GuardBlade)) return;
  138         event.setBlockedDamage(event.getOriginalBlockedDamage() * 0.5F);
  139         event.setShieldTakesDamage(false); // The sword is not a ShieldItem; charge one durability explicitly.
  140         if (event.getBlockedDamage() <= 0) return;
  141         player.getUseItem().hurtAndBreak(1, player, p -> p.broadcastBreakEvent(p.getUsedItemHand()));
  142         if (event.getDamageSource().getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity attacker && hostile(player, attacker)) {
  143             State s = state(player);
  144             s.held = weapon(player);
  145             s.counterUntil = now(player) + SchoolCombatRules.counterWindow(has(player, "zhenguan_mirror"));
  146             cue(player, "guard_ready");
  147         }
  148     }
  149 
  150     @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.LOW)
  151     public static void hurt(LivingHurtEvent event) {
  152         if (DynastyTrinketOnHit.isSyntheticDamage() || event.isCanceled() || event.getAmount() <= 0 || event.getEntity().level().isClientSide
  153                 || !(event.getSource().getEntity() instanceof Player player)) return;
  154         LivingEntity target = event.getEntity();
  155         if (!hostile(player, target)) return;
  156         if (isStarArrow(event.getSource())) {
  157             starHit(player, target, (AbstractArrow) event.getSource().getDirectEntity(), event);
  158             return;
  159         }
  160         // Do not trigger on swept secondaries, thorns, trinket lightning, bow hits or synthetic recursive damage.
  161         if (!event.getSource().is(DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK) || event.getSource().getDirectEntity() != player) return;
  162         State s = STATES.get(player.getUUID());
  163         if (s == null || s.attackTick != now(player) || !target.getUUID().equals(s.attacked)) return;
  164         s.attacked = null; // exactly once, and only the explicit attack target
  165         String held = weapon(player);
  166         if (!held.equals(s.attackWeapon) || !SchoolCombatRules.fullAttack(s.attackStrength) || !player.hasLineOfSight(target)) return;
  167         if ((held.equals("zhenyue_blade") || held.equals("beichen_spear")) && s.counterUntil >= now(player)) {
  168             s.counterUntil = -1;
  169             event.setAmount(event.getAmount() * 1.35F);
  170             if (has(player, "huben_bracer")) {
  171                 if (boss(target)) target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, 40, 0));
  172                 else target.knockback(0.7, player.getX() - target.getX(), player.getZ() - target.getZ());
  173             }
  174             trial(player, "guard");
  175             impact(target);
  176         } else if (held.equals("liuyun_sword")) {
  177             s.combo = SchoolCombatRules.combo(s.combo, target.getUUID().equals(s.comboTarget), now(player) - s.comboTick,
  178                     SchoolCombatRules.comboWindow(has(player, "liancheng_tassel")), s.attackStrength);
  179             s.comboTarget = target.getUUID();
  180             s.comboTick = now(player);
  181             if (s.combo == 3) {
  182                 s.combo = 0;
  183                 event.setAmount(event.getAmount() * 1.5F);
  184                 if (has(player, "tayun_pendant")) player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(DynastyEffects.SWIFT_WIND.get(), 40, 0));
  185                 trial(player, "sword");
  186                 impact(target);
  187             } else cue(player, "combo_" + s.combo);
  188         } else if (held.equals("chiling_brush") && s.edictUntil >= now(player)) {
  189             s.edictUntil = -1;
  190             event.setAmount(event.getAmount() * 1.5F);
  191             if (s.edictHadTarget) trial(player, "talisman");
  192             s.edictHadTarget = false;
  193             impact(target);
  194         }
  195     }
  196 
  197     private static void starHit(Player player, LivingEntity target, AbstractArrow arrow, LivingHurtEvent event) {
  198         if (!arrow.isCritArrow() || arrow.getOwner() != player || arrow.getPersistentData().getBoolean(PROCESSED)) return;
  199         arrow.getPersistentData().putBoolean(PROCESSED, true);
  200         State s = state(player);
  201         if (target.getUUID().equals(s.marked) && SchoolCombatRules.within(now(player), s.markedTick, SchoolCombatRules.MARK_WINDOW)) {
  202             s.marked = null;
  203             event.setAmount(event.getAmount() * 1.4F);
  204             if (has(player, "mingxian_ring")) target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, boss(target) ? 20 : 40, 0));
  205             trial(player, "archer");
  206             impact(target);
  207         } else {
  208             s.marked = target.getUUID();
  209             s.markedTick = now(player);
  210             if (has(player, "guanxing_pendant")) target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.GLOWING, 60, 0));
  211             cue(player, "star_marked");
  212         }
  213     }
  214 
  215     static int edictWindow(Player player) {
  216         return SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_WINDOW + (DynastySchoolProgression.equippedSynergy(player,"talisman") ? SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_WINDOW/5 : 0);
  217     }
  218     static void finishSpellCharge(Player player,boolean hadTarget) {
  219         String held=weapon(player);
  220         if(!Set.of("chiling_brush","leifu_staff").contains(held))return;
  221         State s=state(player);
  222         if(!held.equals(s.held))resetOnSwap(player,s,held);
  223         s.edictUntil=now(player)+edictWindow(player);s.edictHadTarget=hadTarget;cue(player,"edict_ready");
  224     }
  225     static float consumeSpellWindow(Player player) {
  226         State s=STATES.get(player.getUUID());String held=weapon(player);
  227         if(s==null || !held.equals(s.held) || !Set.of("chiling_brush","leifu_staff").contains(held) || s.edictUntil<now(player))return 1;
  228         s.edictUntil=-1;if(s.edictHadTarget)trial(player,"talisman");s.edictHadTarget=false;return 1.5F;
  229     }
  230     static int startEdict(Player player) {
  231         if (player.level().isClientSide || !weapon(player).equals("chiling_brush")
  232                 || player.getCooldowns().isOnCooldown(DynastyWeapons.CHILING_BRUSH.get())) return 0;
  233         State s = state(player);
  234         s.held = weapon(player);
  235         s.origin = player.position();
  236         s.chargeStart = now(player);
  237         s.edictUntil = -1;
  238         player.getCooldowns().addCooldown(DynastyWeapons.CHILING_BRUSH.get(), com.dynasty.expansion.EquipmentBehaviors.cooldown(player,SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_COOLDOWN));
  239         boolean seal = has(player, "sitian_seal");
  240         var targets = player.level().getEntitiesOfClass(LivingEntity.class,
  241                 player.getBoundingBox().inflate(SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_RADIUS),
  242                 target -> hostile(player, target) && player.distanceToSqr(target) <= 25 && player.hasLineOfSight(target));
  243         targets.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(player::distanceToSqr));
  244         int count = Math.min(SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_LIMIT, targets.size());
  245         for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  246             LivingEntity target = targets.get(i);
  247             target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, SchoolCombatRules.edictDuration(boss(target), seal), boss(target) ? 0 : 1));
  248         }
  249         s.edictHadTarget = count > 0;
  250         cue(player, "edict_cast");
  251         player.level().playSound(null, player.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.ENCHANTMENT_TABLE_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6F, 1.25F);
  252         return count;
  253     }
  254 
  255     static void chargeEdict(Player player) {
  256         State s = STATES.get(player.getUUID());
  257         if (s == null || s.chargeStart < 0) return;
  258         if (!charging(player, s)) { endCharge(player); return; }
  259         silkResistance(player, has(player, "dingfeng_silk"));
  260         if (now(player) - s.chargeStart >= SchoolCombatRules.EDICT_CHARGE) {
  261             s.edictUntil = now(player) + edictWindow(player);
  262             endCharge(player);
  263             player.stopUsingItem();
  264             cue(player, "edict_ready");
  265         }
  266     }
  267     private static boolean charging(Player player, State s) {
  268         return player.isUsingItem() && player.getUsedItemHand() == net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND
  269                 && player.getUseItem().getItem() instanceof DynastySchoolWeapons.EdictBrush
  270                 && s.origin != null && player.position().distanceToSqr(s.origin) <= SchoolCombatRules.STATIONARY_DISTANCE_SQUARED;
  271     }
  272     static void endCharge(Player player) {
  273         State s = STATES.get(player.getUUID());
  274         if (s != null) s.chargeStart = -1;
  275         silkResistance(player, false);
  276     }
  277     private static void silkResistance(Player player, boolean enable) {
  278         var attribute = player.getAttribute(Attributes.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE);
  279         if (attribute == null) return;
  280         if (enable && attribute.getModifier(SILK_RESISTANCE) == null)
  281             attribute.addTransientModifier(new AttributeModifier(SILK_RESISTANCE, "dynasty_edict_charge", 0.5, AttributeModifier.Operation.ADDITION));
  282         else if (!enable) attribute.removeModifier(SILK_RESISTANCE);
  283     }
  284     @SubscribeEvent public static void tick(TickEvent.PlayerTickEvent event) {
  285         Player player = event.player;
  286         if (event.phase != TickEvent.Phase.END || player.level().isClientSide) return;
  287         State s = STATES.get(player.getUUID());
  288         if (s == null) return;
  289         String held = weapon(player);
  290         if (!held.equals(s.held)) resetOnSwap(player, s, held);
  291         if (s.chargeStart >= 0 && !charging(player, s)) endCharge(player);
  292         if (s.chargeStart < 0 || !has(player, "dingfeng_silk")) silkResistance(player, false);
  293     }
  294     private static void resetOnSwap(Player player, State s, String held) {
  295         s.held = held;
  296         s.combo = 0;
  297         s.comboTarget = null;
  298         s.counterUntil = -1;
  299         s.edictUntil = -1;
  300         s.attacked = null;
  301         endCharge(player);
  302         // A projectile already in flight retains its one-target mark even after a weapon swap.
  303     }
  304     static void forget(Player player) { silkResistance(player, false); STATES.remove(player.getUUID()); }
  305     @SubscribeEvent public static void logout(PlayerEvent.PlayerLoggedOutEvent event) { forget(event.getEntity()); }
  306     @SubscribeEvent public static void dimension(PlayerEvent.PlayerChangedDimensionEvent event) { forget(event.getEntity()); }
  307     @SubscribeEvent public static void death(LivingDeathEvent event) { if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) forget(player); }
  308     @SubscribeEvent public static void stop(ServerStoppedEvent event) { STATES.clear(); }
  309     private static void cue(Player player, String name) {
  310         player.displayClientMessage(net.minecraft.network.chat.Component.translatable("message.dynasty.school." + name), true);
  311     }
  312     private static void impact(LivingEntity target) {
  313         if (target.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)
  314             level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ENCHANTED_HIT, target.getX(), target.getY() + target.getBbHeight() * .6, target.getZ(), 8, .25, .3, .25, .02);
  315     }
  316     private static void trial(Player player, String school) {
  317         // The introductory milestone is now awarded by a school kill in DynastySchoolProgression.
  318         // Technique effects remain available without being a progression requirement.
  319     }
  320 }

   39   frameSerial=6366 tick=7648 partial=0.7400713，原残影、心粒子与通用VFX可见，专用TangWind实际像素为0；不得用通用绿光当该专用尾迹通过。
   40 run-05/huan_shou_dao-clear-wall-sand-f69-t89.png
   41   frameSerial=69 tick=89 partial=0.18000579，实体确有离地，沙色小块可辨；牛身/玩家遮住大部分弧光，不能仅据1367像素认定弧光完整清晰。
   42 run-05/juque_sword-first-mobile-f388-t525.png
   43   frameSerial=388 tick=525 partial=0.26000953，原残影和原版白扫击占主画面，专项弧光不易分辨。
   44 run-05/seven_star_saber-guard-first-f4514-t4721.png
   45   frameSerial=4514 tick=4721 partial=0.12005973，左侧星芒可辨，但大量原版伤害心粒子遮住受击者；不足以通过七次依序完整观感。
   46 run-05/dragon-part-flight-f6073-t6138.png
   47   frameSerial=6073 tick=6138 partial=0.14007998，真实末影龙头部事件/partSlot=1存在，但C2S记录中心距离6.377707且龙头离开合适画幅；渲染像素区域被左上小地图覆盖，不能当飞行部位可辨识验收。
   48 run-04/qinggang_sword-side-night-10fps-f1337-t1410.png
   49   frameSerial=1337 tick=1410 partial=0.32002306，协调独立抽查：淡青短痕存在，原版心粒子/白月牙遮挡辨识，保留质量问题。
   50 run-04/xuantian_axe-third-glass-f5858-t7137.png
   51   frameSerial=5858 tick=7137 partial=0.50006056，协调独立抽查：头侧后裂纹可见，被玩家躯干和心粒子局部遮挡；不将nonzero表述为完整优质或全方向可见。
   52 run-04/tianlang_bow-visible-third-10fps-f10508-t10825.png
   53   frameSerial=10508 tick=10825 partial=0.36007547，协调独立抽查：白蓝流星尾迹明显可辨。仅用于原生发射后尾迹；不能证明持续持弓观感。服务端该刻held=tianlang_bow；持握外观辨识有歧义，视觉观察不覆盖源记录。此图只验流星箭尾迹，不验持弓外观。
   54 
   55 覆盖边界：
   56 所有150场景以独立整包86jar/96mod实例运行；run03/04/05均正常退出0。未写资产、正式Java、原清单或用户实例。
   57 原生MultiPlayerGameMode.attack/useItem/releaseUsingItem→实际服务器事件→正式反馈包→实际渲染→同frameSerial的PNG；没有直接假发反馈包。
   58 截图是RenderTick.END实际framebuffer；像素差是专项renderer调用前后读回，最终HUD可能再遮住效果，因此最终PNG肉眼判断独立保留。计数不是质量标准。
   59 run03及run04旧wall/glass平面与目标bbox相交，不能当干净遮挡正例；run05移至x=-2补测。旧ceiling有0.6空隙不能当零抬升，run05 top-slab紧顶棚补负例。
   60 run05/tang-clear-flags仍有上一场景向外扩散的水；其去除速度判定有效，不能称干燥地面去除场景。
   61 10FPS是请求上限，实际frameIntervalMs逐帧记录；像素读回增加开销，不用于未插桩性能认证。
   62 原生伤害导致大量原版心形粒子保留，没有为通过而禁粒子、隐藏身体、改VFX、修改资产。
   63 双客户端32边界由Herschel负责；本报告不冒充真人双人网络验收。未覆盖的新机制/资产条目不关闭。
   64 
   65 机器索引：各run/case-index.json；全部geometry/像素/同帧screen在*-frames.json；原生输入、伤害、箭矢、S2C在*-events.json。gallery.html只供定位，自动选图不代表通过。
   66 
   67 追加采集审计：capture-quality-audit.json列出22张旧截图的world receipt tick与capture tick不一致，不用于精确同帧断言。本报告指定关键PNG已逐一通过tick对照。此前玉笛被独立抽看的正向画面仍有效；不要用旧清除末帧f5348-t5448作同帧解除证明。
